// Last Call data model.
// Conventions: snake_case tables/columns, integer cents for money, all timestamps stored in UTC (timestamptz).
// Ownership split: venue_* tables hold venue-controlled facts; report/photo/comment/presence/rsvp hold user reality.
// There is deliberately NO accuracy score column anywhere: it is always computed from data (invariant 4).

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider  = "postgresql"
  /// Pooled connection for the app (Neon: the "-pooler" host with ?pgbouncer=true). Local: same as DIRECT_URL.
  url       = env("DATABASE_URL")
  /// Direct (unpooled) connection, used by `prisma migrate`.
  directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")
}

enum Role {
  USER
  VENUE_OWNER
  MODERATOR
  ADMIN
}

enum SessionClient {
  WEB
  IOS
}

enum ReportType {
  VIBE
  LINE
  COVER
}

enum Vibe {
  PACKED
  BUSY
  CHILL
  DEAD
}

enum LineWait {
  NONE
  UNDER_10
  TEN_TO_30
  OVER_30
}

enum ClaimStatus {
  PENDING
  APPROVED
  REJECTED
}

enum ContentType {
  PHOTO
  COMMENT
  REPORT
  /// A user account (profile reports; suspension decisions).
  USER
}

enum FlagStatus {
  OPEN
  RESOLVED
}

enum ModerationAction {
  FLAGGED
  AUTO_HIDE
  HIDE
  DISMISS
  /// Moderator reviewed hidden content (e.g. auto-hidden after 3 flags) and restored it.
  RESTORE
  /// System: the author deleted their account, so their personal photos were hidden.
  ACCOUNT_DELETED
  /// Moderator removed an abusive account (can't sign in; sessions revoked).
  SUSPEND
  UNSUSPEND
}

enum VerificationAction {
  GRANT
  REVOKE
}

model User {
  id                  String    @id @default(cuid())
  email               String    @unique
  handle              String    @unique
  displayName         String    @map("display_name")
  passwordHash        String    @map("password_hash")
  role                Role      @default(USER)
  /// Reputation weight in [0.2, 1.5]; new accounts start at 0.5. Written only by the reputation job.
  reputation          Float     @default(0.5)
  reputationUpdatedAt DateTime? @map("reputation_updated_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  createdAt           DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Set when the account was deleted (anonymized). The row stays so reports/comments keep their history.
  deletedAt           DateTime? @map("deleted_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  termsAcceptedAt     DateTime? @map("terms_accepted_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  termsVersion        String?   @map("terms_version")
  /// App Review / QA accounts: may post from anywhere, but their reports, photos, presence and RSVPs are
  /// stored and excluded from consensus, counts, accuracy and reputation. Settable only by an admin or the seed.
  geofenceExempt      Boolean   @default(false) @map("geofence_exempt")
  /// Set by a moderator for abusive accounts: can't sign in, all sessions revoked. Content stays until moderated.
  suspendedAt         DateTime? @map("suspended_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Private accounts (the default for every account): only approved followers see the profile, and on venue
  /// pages their photos and comments read "Anonymous" to everyone else. See src/server/privacy.ts.
  isPrivate           Boolean   @default(true) @map("is_private")
  /// Max 150 characters, content-filtered.
  bio                 String    @default("")
  /// Profile photo (re-encoded square JPEG). Shown only to viewers allowed to see the full profile.
  avatarKey           String?   @map("avatar_key")
  /// Verified badge, handpicked by an admin. Every grant and revoke is in the append-only verification_event log.
  /// Badge only: it never changes ranking, featuring, consensus or anything else.
  verifiedAt          DateTime? @map("verified_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  sessions               Session[]
  claims                 VenueClaim[]        @relation("claimant")
  reviewedClaims         VenueClaim[]        @relation("claimReviewer")
  reports                Report[]
  photos                 Photo[]
  comments               Comment[]
  presence               Presence[]
  rsvps                  Rsvp[]
  following              Follow[]            @relation("follower")
  followers              Follow[]            @relation("followee")
  flags                  Flag[]
  moderationEvents       ModerationEvent[]
  promosCreated          Promo[]
  coverChanges           VenueCoverHistory[]
  blocking               Block[]             @relation("blocker")
  blockedBy              Block[]             @relation("blocked")
  followRequestsSent     FollowRequest[]     @relation("requester")
  followRequestsReceived FollowRequest[]     @relation("requestTarget")
  likes                  PhotoLike[]
  verificationEvents     VerificationEvent[] @relation("verifiedUser")
  verificationActions    VerificationEvent[] @relation("verifier")
  rateEvents             RateEvent[]

  @@map("app_user")
}

model Session {
  /// sha256 of the random token stored in the cookie; the raw token never touches the DB.
  id        String        @id
  userId    String        @map("user_id")
  /// WEB sessions ride in an httpOnly cookie; IOS sessions are bearer tokens kept in the iOS keychain.
  client    SessionClient @default(WEB)
  expiresAt DateTime      @map("expires_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  createdAt DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
  @@map("session")
}

model Venue {
  id                 String   @id @default(cuid())
  slug               String   @unique
  name               String
  address            String
  neighborhood       String
  lat                Float
  lng                Float
  /// Weekly hours: [{ day: 0-6 (Sun-Sat), open: "HH:MM", close: "HH:MM" }] in America/New_York wall time.
  hours              Json
  /// Official cover in cents. null = venue has not stated a cover. 0 = free.
  officialCoverCents Int?     @map("official_cover_cents")
  dressCode          String?  @map("dress_code")
  officialPhotoKey   String?  @map("official_photo_key")
  /// OpenStreetMap element ("node/123", "way/456") for imported venues (ODbL: attribute "© OpenStreetMap contributors").
  osmId              String?  @unique @map("osm_id")
  createdAt          DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  updatedAt          DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  claims       VenueClaim[]
  promos       Promo[]
  coverHistory VenueCoverHistory[]
  reports      Report[]
  photos       Photo[]
  comments     Comment[]
  presence     Presence[]
  rsvps        Rsvp[]
  flags        Flag[]
  featured     FeaturedPhoto[]

  @@map("venue")
}

/// Append-only history of the official cover so the accuracy score can compare
/// each past hour against what the venue was claiming at that time.
model VenueCoverHistory {
  id            String   @id @default(cuid())
  venueId       String   @map("venue_id")
  coverCents    Int?     @map("cover_cents")
  effectiveFrom DateTime @map("effective_from") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  changedById   String?  @map("changed_by_id")
  venue         Venue    @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  changedBy     User?    @relation(fields: [changedById], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([venueId, effectiveFrom])
  @@map("venue_cover_history")
}

/// Ownership link. A VENUE_OWNER owns exactly the venues with an APPROVED claim.
model VenueClaim {
  id           String      @id @default(cuid())
  venueId      String      @map("venue_id")
  userId       String      @map("user_id")
  status       ClaimStatus @default(PENDING)
  reviewedById String?     @map("reviewed_by_id")
  reviewedAt   DateTime?   @map("reviewed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  createdAt    DateTime    @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  venue        Venue       @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  user         User        @relation("claimant", fields: [userId], references: [id])
  reviewedBy   User?       @relation("claimReviewer", fields: [reviewedById], references: [id])

  @@unique([venueId, userId])
  @@index([userId, status])
  @@map("venue_claim")
}

model Promo {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  venueId     String   @map("venue_id")
  title       String
  body        String
  startsAt    DateTime @map("starts_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  endsAt      DateTime @map("ends_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Set by ADMIN only. Paid promos are always labeled "Promoted" in the UI.
  isPaid      Boolean  @default(false) @map("is_paid")
  createdById String   @map("created_by_id")
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  venue       Venue    @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  createdBy   User     @relation(fields: [createdById], references: [id])

  @@index([venueId, endsAt])
  @@map("promo")
}

model Report {
  id         String     @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String     @map("user_id")
  venueId    String     @map("venue_id")
  type       ReportType
  vibe       Vibe?
  line       LineWait?
  coverCents Int?       @map("cover_cents")
  /// Coordinates the client submitted; kept for auditing the geofence check.
  lat        Float
  lng        Float
  createdAt  DateTime   @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  hiddenAt   DateTime?  @map("hidden_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Snapshot at write time: false when posted by a geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) account. Aggregates
  /// only use countable rows, so later changes to the author's flag (or deleting the account) can't
  /// retroactively add or remove data.
  countable  Boolean    @default(true)
  user       User       @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  venue      Venue      @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])

  @@index([venueId, type, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, venueId, type, createdAt])
  @@map("report")
}

/// A photo taken at a venue. A photo **with a rating is a post**: it also appears on the author's profile, can be
/// liked (when shared) and featured. A photo without a rating is a live-only photo, as before.
model Photo {
  id         String         @id @default(cuid())
  userId     String         @map("user_id")
  venueId    String         @map("venue_id")
  storageKey String         @unique @map("storage_key")
  mimeType   String         @map("mime_type")
  caption    String
  lat        Float
  lng        Float
  createdAt  DateTime       @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  hiddenAt   DateTime?      @map("hidden_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// 1 to 10 (CHECK). null = live-only photo. Editable until the end of the night it was taken; never cleared once set.
  rating     Int?           @db.SmallInt
  /// Shown in the venue's photo feed (and eligible for likes and featuring). Unshared posts appear only on the profile. Defaults off (fails closed); the service sets it: on for public accounts and live-only photos, off for private accounts.
  shared     Boolean        @default(false)
  /// The America/New_York night (YYYY-MM-DD, 5am rollover) of created_at. Set by a database trigger on insert.
  night      String         @default("")
  editedAt   DateTime?      @map("edited_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// The author removed it from their profile ("delete" means hide). Final: a trigger refuses to clear it.
  removedAt  DateTime?      @map("removed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Snapshot at write time: false when posted by a geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) account. Aggregates
  /// only use countable rows, so later changes to the author's flag (or deleting the account) can't
  /// retroactively add or remove data.
  countable  Boolean        @default(true)
  user       User           @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  venue      Venue          @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  likes      PhotoLike[]
  featured   FeaturedPhoto?

  @@index([venueId, createdAt])
  @@index([venueId, night])
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, venueId, night])
  @@map("photo")
}

model Comment {
  id           String    @id @default(cuid())
  venueId      String    @map("venue_id")
  userId       String    @map("user_id")
  /// Set only for venue replies; replies are one level deep under a user comment.
  parentId     String?   @map("parent_id")
  isVenueReply Boolean   @default(false) @map("is_venue_reply")
  body         String
  createdAt    DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  editedAt     DateTime? @map("edited_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  hiddenAt     DateTime? @map("hidden_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// false when posted by a geofence-exempt account: then visible only to its author.
  countable    Boolean   @default(true)
  venue        Venue     @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  user         User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  parent       Comment?  @relation("replies", fields: [parentId], references: [id])
  replies      Comment[] @relation("replies")

  @@index([venueId, createdAt])
  @@map("comment")
}

/// "Here now". One row per user (you can only be in one place). Counts only if last_seen_at is within 45 minutes.
model Presence {
  userId     String   @id @map("user_id")
  venueId    String   @map("venue_id")
  lastSeenAt DateTime @map("last_seen_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  countable  Boolean  @default(true)
  user       User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  venue      Venue    @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])

  @@index([venueId, lastSeenAt])
  @@map("presence")
}

/// "Going tonight". Keyed by night (YYYY-MM-DD of the 5am-to-4:59am America/New_York night), so it resets each night.
model Rsvp {
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  venueId   String   @map("venue_id")
  night     String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  countable Boolean  @default(true)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  venue     Venue    @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, venueId, night])
  @@index([venueId, night])
  @@map("rsvp")
}

model Follow {
  followerId String   @map("follower_id")
  followeeId String   @map("followee_id")
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  follower   User     @relation("follower", fields: [followerId], references: [id])
  followee   User     @relation("followee", fields: [followeeId], references: [id])

  @@id([followerId, followeeId])
  @@index([followeeId])
  @@map("follow")
}

/// A pending request to follow a private account. Approving it creates the follow row (every follow row is an
/// approved follow), declining or cancelling deletes the request.
model FollowRequest {
  requesterId String   @map("requester_id")
  targetId    String   @map("target_id")
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  requester   User     @relation("requester", fields: [requesterId], references: [id])
  target      User     @relation("requestTarget", fields: [targetId], references: [id])

  @@id([requesterId, targetId])
  @@index([targetId, createdAt])
  @@map("follow_request")
}

/// One like per user per shared post. Likes are reactions, not content: unliking deletes the row.
/// Likes never affect consensus, counts, accuracy or reputation; only eligible ones count toward featuring.
model PhotoLike {
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  photoId   String   @map("photo_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Write-time snapshot: false for geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) accounts, whose likes never count.
  countable Boolean  @default(true)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  photo     Photo    @relation(fields: [photoId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, photoId])
  @@index([photoId, createdAt])
  @@map("photo_like")
}

/// Nightly featuring result: the top 20 shared posts of a venue's night by eligible likes (at least 3), with their
/// rank. Append-only (trigger). The gallery shows the top 10 that are still visible.
model FeaturedPhoto {
  id            String      @id @default(cuid())
  venueId       String      @map("venue_id")
  night         String
  photoId       String      @unique @map("photo_id")
  rank          Int         @db.SmallInt
  eligibleLikes Int         @map("eligible_likes")
  createdAt     DateTime    @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  venue         Venue       @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  photo         Photo       @relation(fields: [photoId], references: [id])
  run           FeaturedRun @relation(fields: [night], references: [night])

  @@unique([venueId, night, rank])
  @@map("featured_photo")
}

/// One row per night the featuring job has processed, written in the same transaction as its featured_photo rows,
/// so a rerun or a late run never features a night twice. Append-only (trigger).
model FeaturedRun {
  night    String          @id
  ranAt    DateTime        @map("ran_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  featured Int
  photos   FeaturedPhoto[]

  @@map("featured_run")
}

/// Append-only log of verified-badge grants and revokes (trigger rejects UPDATE and DELETE). A note is required.
model VerificationEvent {
  id        String             @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String             @map("user_id")
  action    VerificationAction
  note      String
  /// null = system (e.g. the account was deleted).
  actorId   String?            @map("actor_id")
  createdAt DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User               @relation("verifiedUser", fields: [userId], references: [id])
  actor     User?              @relation("verifier", fields: [actorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@map("verification_event")
}

/// Rate-limit ledger for social actions (follows and requests, likes, posts). Counted under a per-user advisory
/// lock; rows older than a day are pruned by the cron job.
model RateEvent {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  kind      String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])

  @@index([userId, kind, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
  @@map("rate_event")
}

model Flag {
  id         String      @id @default(cuid())
  targetType ContentType @map("target_type")
  targetId   String      @map("target_id")
  /// null for USER targets (profile reports).
  venueId    String?     @map("venue_id")
  flaggerId  String      @map("flagger_id")
  reason     String
  status     FlagStatus  @default(OPEN)
  createdAt  DateTime    @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  flagger    User        @relation(fields: [flaggerId], references: [id])
  venue      Venue?      @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])

  /// One OPEN flag per user per item (partial unique index in migration 3); after a review you may flag again.
  @@index([targetType, targetId, flaggerId])
  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@map("flag")
}

/// Append-only audit log. UPDATE and DELETE are rejected by a database trigger (see migration).
model ModerationEvent {
  id         String           @id @default(cuid())
  action     ModerationAction
  targetType ContentType      @map("target_type")
  targetId   String           @map("target_id")
  /// null = system action (e.g. auto-hide after 3 distinct flags)
  actorId    String?          @map("actor_id")
  note       String?
  createdAt  DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  actor      User?            @relation(fields: [actorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([targetType, targetId])
  @@map("moderation_event")
}

/// Failed login attempts, for the email+IP throttle. Rows older than a day are pruned by the cron job.
model LoginAttempt {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  email     String
  ip        String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  @@index([email, ip, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
  @@map("login_attempt")
}

/// A blocks B. Symmetric in effect: neither sees the other's photos, comments or profile, and neither can
/// follow the other. Aggregates (consensus, counts, reputation) are unaffected.
model Block {
  blockerId String   @map("blocker_id")
  blockedId String   @map("blocked_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Opaque id for the block list, so a block made from an "Anonymous" photo or comment can be undone without
  /// ever revealing who it is.
  ref       String   @unique @default(dbgenerated("(gen_random_uuid())::text"))
  /// Where the block came from: "profile" (the blocker saw the handle), "photo" or "comment" (the author may have
  /// been Anonymous to the blocker, so the block list never shows their handle).
  source    String   @default("profile")
  blocker   User     @relation("blocker", fields: [blockerId], references: [id])
  blocked   User     @relation("blocked", fields: [blockedId], references: [id])

  @@id([blockerId, blockedId])
  @@index([blockedId])
  @@map("block")
}

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Hand-written guarantees (invariant 5): moderation_event is append-only and
-- user content is hidden, never deleted. Row-level DELETE/UPDATE triggers do
-- not fire on TRUNCATE, so `prisma migrate reset` and the test harness still work.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: % on % is not allowed (%)', TG_OP, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_ARGV[0]
    USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER moderation_event_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "moderation_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('moderation log is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER moderation_event_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "moderation_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('moderation log is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER venue_cover_history_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "venue_cover_history"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('cover history is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER venue_cover_history_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "venue_cover_history"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('cover history is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER report_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "report"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');
CREATE TRIGGER photo_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');
CREATE TRIGGER comment_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "comment"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('hide user content instead of deleting it');

-- Once hidden, content stays hidden (un-hiding would need its own audited action; not in scope).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.hidden_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.hidden_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.hidden_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: hidden_at on % cannot be cleared or changed', TG_TABLE_NAME
      USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER report_hidden_sticky BEFORE UPDATE ON "report" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_hidden_sticky BEFORE UPDATE ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();
CREATE TRIGGER comment_hidden_sticky BEFORE UPDATE ON "comment" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();

-- Sanity checks on values.
ALTER TABLE "app_user" ADD CONSTRAINT app_user_reputation_range CHECK (reputation >= 0.2 AND reputation <= 1.5);
ALTER TABLE "report" ADD CONSTRAINT report_value_matches_type CHECK (
  (type = 'VIBE'  AND vibe IS NOT NULL AND line IS NULL AND cover_cents IS NULL) OR
  (type = 'LINE'  AND line IS NOT NULL AND vibe IS NULL AND cover_cents IS NULL) OR
  (type = 'COVER' AND cover_cents IS NOT NULL AND cover_cents >= 0 AND vibe IS NULL AND line IS NULL)
);
ALTER TABLE "venue" ADD CONSTRAINT venue_official_cover_nonneg CHECK (official_cover_cents IS NULL OR official_cover_cents >= 0);
ALTER TABLE "promo" ADD CONSTRAINT promo_window CHECK (ends_at > starts_at);

-- AlterEnum
ALTER TYPE "ModerationAction" ADD VALUE 'RESTORE';

-- DropForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "moderation_event" DROP CONSTRAINT "moderation_event_actor_id_fkey";

-- DropForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "venue_cover_history" DROP CONSTRAINT "venue_cover_history_changed_by_id_fkey";

-- AddForeignKey

-- AddForeignKey
ALTER TABLE "flag" ADD CONSTRAINT "flag_venue_id_fkey" FOREIGN KEY ("venue_id") REFERENCES "venue"("id") ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE;

-- AddForeignKey

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Moderators must be able to restore auto-hidden content after review, so hidden_at is no
-- longer sticky at the DB level. Every hide/restore goes through the moderation service,
-- which writes an append-only moderation_event in the same transaction.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS report_hidden_sticky ON "report";
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS photo_hidden_sticky ON "photo";
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS comment_hidden_sticky ON "comment";
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS lastcall_hidden_is_sticky();

-- User content is immutable apart from moderation state (and a comment author's own edits).
-- Protects authorship, venue, timestamps and values: e.g. nobody can bump a photo's created_at
-- to put a stale photo back into the live feed (invariant 6) or rewrite a report's value.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_report_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.type, NEW.vibe, NEW.line, NEW.cover_cents, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.type, OLD.vibe, OLD.line, OLD.cover_cents, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: reports are immutable except hidden_at' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.storage_key, NEW.mime_type, NEW.caption, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.storage_key, OLD.mime_type, OLD.caption, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: photos are immutable except hidden_at' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_comment_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.parent_id, NEW.is_venue_reply, NEW.created_at)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.parent_id, OLD.is_venue_reply, OLD.created_at) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only body/edited_at/hidden_at of a comment can change' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER report_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "report" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_report_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER comment_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON "comment" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_comment_immutable();

ALTER TABLE "comment" ADD CONSTRAINT comment_reply_shape CHECK (is_venue_reply = (parent_id IS NOT NULL));

-- The snapshot is part of the immutable record for reports and photos: once written it never changes.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.countable IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.countable THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: countable on % is a write-time snapshot and cannot change', TG_TABLE_NAME USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Hand-written guarantees for the social layer.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Every account starts private, including existing ones: the column default (true) applies to every existing row.
-- Every existing follow is an approved follow: requests live in follow_request, so no follow rows change.

-- A photo's night (America/New_York, 5am rollover) is derived from created_at on insert, so featuring and
-- Regular status can never disagree with the timestamp. created_at is immutable, so night is too.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_night_of(ts timestamptz) RETURNS text AS $$
  SELECT to_char((ts AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York') - interval '5 hours', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_set_night() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.night := lastcall_night_of(NEW.created_at);
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER photo_set_night BEFORE INSERT ON "photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_set_night();

-- Backfill existing photos. The immutability trigger below is replaced after this, and night isn't in the old one.
UPDATE "photo" SET night = lastcall_night_of(created_at);

ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_rating_range CHECK (rating IS NULL OR (rating BETWEEN 1 AND 10));
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_night_set CHECK (night <> '');

-- Photos stay immutable except moderation state and the author's post fields:
--  - caption, shared, edited_at: the author's edits (the service freezes the caption while a flag is open);
--  - rating: may be added or changed only until the end of the photo's night, and never cleared once set;
--  - removed_at: the author removing it from their profile, final once set;
--  - hidden_at: moderation (logged in moderation_event by the service).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_photo_immutable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.id, NEW.user_id, NEW.venue_id, NEW.storage_key, NEW.mime_type, NEW.lat, NEW.lng, NEW.created_at, NEW.night)
     IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.id, OLD.user_id, OLD.venue_id, OLD.storage_key, OLD.mime_type, OLD.lat, OLD.lng, OLD.created_at, OLD.night) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: photos are immutable except moderation state and the author''s post fields' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.rating IS NOT NULL AND NEW.rating IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a post''s rating can''t be cleared' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.rating IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.rating
     AND clock_timestamp() >= ((OLD.night::date + 1) + time '05:00') AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a post''s rating is locked after its night ends' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.removed_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.removed_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.removed_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a removed post stays removed' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- A like is only for someone else's shared post.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT user_id, rating, shared INTO p FROM "photo" WHERE id = NEW.photo_id;
  IF p.user_id = NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: you can''t like your own post' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.rating IS NULL OR NOT p.shared THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only shared posts can be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER photo_like_guard BEFORE INSERT ON "photo_like" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard();
CREATE TRIGGER photo_like_countable_immutable BEFORE UPDATE OF countable ON "photo_like" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_countable_immutable();

ALTER TABLE "follow_request" ADD CONSTRAINT follow_request_not_self CHECK (requester_id <> target_id);
ALTER TABLE "follow" ADD CONSTRAINT follow_not_self CHECK (follower_id <> followee_id);
ALTER TABLE "app_user" ADD CONSTRAINT app_user_bio_length CHECK (char_length(bio) <= 150);

-- Featuring results and runs, and the verification log, are append-only.
ALTER TABLE "featured_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT featured_photo_rank_range CHECK (rank BETWEEN 1 AND 20);
ALTER TABLE "featured_photo" ADD CONSTRAINT featured_photo_min_likes CHECK (eligible_likes >= 3);
CREATE TRIGGER featured_photo_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "featured_photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring results are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER featured_photo_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "featured_photo"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring results are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER featured_run_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "featured_run"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring runs are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER featured_run_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "featured_run"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('featuring runs are append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER verification_event_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON "verification_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('verification log is append-only');
CREATE TRIGGER verification_event_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON "verification_event"
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_reject_mutation('verification log is append-only');
ALTER TABLE "verification_event" ADD CONSTRAINT verification_event_note CHECK (char_length(btrim(note)) BETWEEN 3 AND 300);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Review fixes for the social layer (hand-written).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- The end of a night (5am America/New_York the next morning) as an instant.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_night_end(night text) RETURNS timestamptz AS $$
  SELECT ((night::date + 1) + time '05:00') AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York';
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

-- Captions: 280 characters for posts (live-only photos are limited to 140 by the service).
ALTER TABLE "photo" ADD CONSTRAINT photo_caption_length CHECK (char_length(caption) <= 280);

-- Likes: only on someone else's visible, shared post by a live account, and never on App Review posts.
-- FOR SHARE so a concurrent un-share, removal or hide can't interleave with the insert.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_like_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT ph.user_id, ph.rating, ph.shared, ph.hidden_at, ph.removed_at, ph.countable, u.deleted_at
    INTO p
    FROM "photo" ph JOIN "app_user" u ON u.id = ph.user_id
    WHERE ph.id = NEW.photo_id
    FOR SHARE OF ph;
  IF p.user_id = NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: you can''t like your own post' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.rating IS NULL OR NOT p.shared THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only shared posts can be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.hidden_at IS NOT NULL OR p.removed_at IS NOT NULL OR NOT p.countable OR p.deleted_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: this post can''t be liked' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- A night can only be featured once it has ended, and a featured row must match its photo: same venue and night,
-- a shared, visible post. Both tables are append-only, so a bad insert would otherwise be permanent.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_run_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF clock_timestamp() < lastcall_night_end(NEW.night) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: night % hasn''t ended yet', NEW.night USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER featured_run_guard BEFORE INSERT ON "featured_run" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_run_guard();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_photo_guard() RETURNS trigger AS $$
DECLARE p record;
BEGIN
  SELECT venue_id, night, rating, shared, hidden_at, removed_at, countable INTO p FROM "photo" WHERE id = NEW.photo_id;
  IF p.venue_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.venue_id OR p.night IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.night THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: a featured photo must match its venue and night' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  IF p.rating IS NULL OR NOT p.shared OR p.hidden_at IS NOT NULL OR p.removed_at IS NOT NULL OR NOT p.countable THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: only shared, visible posts can be featured' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER featured_photo_guard BEFORE INSERT ON "featured_photo" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_featured_photo_guard();

-- A follow and a pending request for the same pair never coexist: creating the follow clears the request.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_follow_clears_request() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  DELETE FROM "follow_request" WHERE requester_id = NEW.follower_id AND target_id = NEW.followee_id;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER follow_clears_request AFTER INSERT ON "follow" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_follow_clears_request();

ALTER TABLE "rate_event" ADD CONSTRAINT rate_event_kind CHECK (kind IN ('follow', 'like', 'post'));

====================================================================================================
== prisma/migrations/20260927230000_verified_logged/migration.sql
====================================================================================================

-- The verified badge changes only together with its log entry: an UPDATE of app_user.verified_at must be matched by a
-- verification_event for that user, with the right action, written earlier in the same transaction. Whatever path
-- writes the column (the service, a script, a future admin tool), the append-only log can't be skipped.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_verified_needs_event() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.verified_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.verified_at AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM "verification_event" e
    WHERE e.user_id = NEW.id
      AND e.xmin = ((txid_current() % 4294967296)::text)::xid
      AND e.action = (CASE WHEN NEW.verified_at IS NULL THEN 'REVOKE' ELSE 'GRANT' END)::"VerificationAction"
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: the verified badge changes only with a verification log entry' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER app_user_verified_logged BEFORE UPDATE OF verified_at ON "app_user" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_verified_needs_event();

-- A new account can't start verified (there's no log entry yet to match).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION lastcall_no_verified_insert() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.verified_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lastcall: accounts are verified through the verification log, not at creation' USING ERRCODE = 'insufficient_privilege';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER app_user_no_verified_insert BEFORE INSERT ON "app_user" FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION lastcall_no_verified_insert();


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/consensus/index.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Consensus engine. Pure functions only: no DB, no clock. Callers pass `now` and pre-filtered reports
 * (hidden/moderated reports are excluded by the caller).
 *
 * Model
 *   weight(report) = reputation(author) x decay(age)
 *   decay(age)     = 0.5 ^ (age / 30 min), and 0 once age >= 3 h (the live window is the half-open interval (now-3h, now])
 *   vibe, line     = weighted mode
 *   cover          = weighted median (cents)
 *   Only each user's newest report per metric counts, so one person re-reporting every 15 minutes
 *   cannot outvote a crowd. Reports stamped after `now` are ignored, which also lets the reputation job
 *   replay the past.
 *   A value is shown only if the last hour, (now-1h, now], holds >= 2 reports from >= 2 distinct users.
 */
import type { LineWait, Vibe } from "@prisma/client";

export const HALF_LIFE_MS = 30 * 60_000;
export const MAX_AGE_MS = 3 * 60 * 60_000;
export const MIN_DATA_WINDOW_MS = 60 * 60_000;
export const MIN_REPORTS = 2;
export const MIN_DISTINCT_USERS = 2;

export const REPUTATION_MIN = 0.2;
export const REPUTATION_MAX = 1.5;
export const REPUTATION_NEW = 0.5;

/** Ordered scales (most to least, and shortest to longest) used for tie-breaks and "adjacent" agreement. */
export const VIBE_SCALE: readonly Vibe[] = ["PACKED", "BUSY", "CHILL", "DEAD"];
export const LINE_SCALE: readonly LineWait[] = ["NONE", "UNDER_10", "TEN_TO_30", "OVER_30"];

export type ConsensusReport<T> = {
  userId: string;
  value: T;
  createdAt: Date;
  /** Author's reputation weight. Out-of-range values are clamped to [0.2, 1.5]. */
  reputation: number;
};

/** reportCount = live reports in the last 3h; userCount = distinct reporters (only their newest report is weighted). */
export type Consensus<T> =
  | { status: "ok"; value: T; reportCount: number; userCount: number; newestAt: Date; totalWeight: number }
  | { status: "insufficient"; reportCount: number; userCount: number; newestAt: Date | null };

export function clampReputation(r: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(r)) return REPUTATION_NEW;
  return Math.min(REPUTATION_MAX, Math.max(REPUTATION_MIN, r));
}

/** Exponential decay with a 30-minute half-life; zero at 3 hours and beyond, and for future timestamps. */
export function decayWeight(ageMs: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(ageMs) || ageMs < 0 || ageMs >= MAX_AGE_MS) return 0;
  return Math.pow(0.5, ageMs / HALF_LIFE_MS);
}

export function reportWeight(r: { createdAt: Date; reputation: number }, now: Date): number {
  return clampReputation(r.reputation) * decayWeight(now.getTime() - r.createdAt.getTime());
}

/** Reports in the live window (now-3h, now]. */
export function liveReports<T>(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<T>[], now: Date): ConsensusReport<T>[] {
  const t = now.getTime();
  return reports.filter((r) => {
    const age = t - r.createdAt.getTime();
    return age >= 0 && age < MAX_AGE_MS;
  });
}

/** Each user's newest report. Ties on timestamp keep the later one in input order. */
export function latestPerUser<T>(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<T>[]): ConsensusReport<T>[] {
  const byUser = new Map<string, ConsensusReport<T>>();
  for (const r of reports) {
    const prev = byUser.get(r.userId);
    if (!prev || r.createdAt.getTime() >= prev.createdAt.getTime()) byUser.set(r.userId, r);
  }
  return [...byUser.values()];
}

/** The "not enough data" floor: >= 2 reports from >= 2 distinct users within (now-1h, now]. */
export function hasEnoughData(reports: readonly { userId: string; createdAt: Date }[], now: Date): boolean {
  const t = now.getTime();
  const recent = reports.filter((r) => {
    const age = t - r.createdAt.getTime();
    return age >= 0 && age < MIN_DATA_WINDOW_MS;
  });
  return recent.length >= MIN_REPORTS && new Set(recent.map((r) => r.userId)).size >= MIN_DISTINCT_USERS;
}

type Weighted<T> = { value: T; weight: number; createdAt: Date };

/**
 * Weighted mode. Tie-break: the tied value with the most recent report wins. If that also ties, the value
 * earlier in `scale` wins, so the result is deterministic.
 */
export function weightedMode<T>(items: readonly Weighted<T>[], scale: readonly T[]): T | null {
  const totals = new Map<T, { w: number; newest: number }>();
  for (const it of items) {
    if (!(it.weight > 0)) continue;
    const cur = totals.get(it.value) ?? { w: 0, newest: -Infinity };
    cur.w += it.weight;
    cur.newest = Math.max(cur.newest, it.createdAt.getTime());
    totals.set(it.value, cur);
  }
  let best: { value: T; w: number; newest: number } | null = null;
  const EPS = 1e-12;
  for (const [value, { w, newest }] of totals) {
    if (
      !best ||
      w > best.w + EPS ||
      (Math.abs(w - best.w) <= EPS &&
        (newest > best.newest || (newest === best.newest && scale.indexOf(value) < scale.indexOf(best.value))))
    ) {
      best = { value, w, newest };
    }
  }
  return best ? best.value : null;
}

/**
 * Weighted median of integer values. It is the smallest value whose cumulative weight reaches half the
 * total. When the cumulative weight lands exactly on the halfway point, it averages that value with the
 * next one (the textbook even-count median) and rounds to the cent.
 */
export function weightedMedian(items: readonly { value: number; weight: number }[]): number | null {
  const xs = items.filter((i) => i.weight > 0 && Number.isFinite(i.value)).sort((a, b) => a.value - b.value);
  if (!xs.length) return null;
  const total = xs.reduce((s, i) => s + i.weight, 0);
  const half = total / 2;
  const EPS = 1e-9 * total;
  let cum = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
    cum += xs[i]!.weight;
    if (Math.abs(cum - half) <= EPS && i + 1 < xs.length) return Math.round((xs[i]!.value + xs[i + 1]!.value) / 2);
    if (cum > half) return xs[i]!.value;
  }
  return xs[xs.length - 1]!.value;
}

function summarize<T>(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<T>[], now: Date) {
  const live = liveReports(reports, now);
  // Canonical order so floating-point sums (and therefore the result) don't depend on input order.
  const counted = latestPerUser(live).sort((a, b) => (a.userId < b.userId ? -1 : a.userId > b.userId ? 1 : 0));
  const newestAt = live.reduce<Date | null>((m, r) => (!m || r.createdAt > m ? r.createdAt : m), null);
  const weighted = counted.map((r) => ({ value: r.value, createdAt: r.createdAt, weight: reportWeight(r, now) }));
  return { live, counted, newestAt, weighted, enough: hasEnoughData(live, now) };
}

function categorical<T>(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<T>[], now: Date, scale: readonly T[]): Consensus<T> {
  const s = summarize(reports, now);
  const base = { reportCount: s.live.length, userCount: s.counted.length, newestAt: s.newestAt };
  const value = s.enough ? weightedMode(s.weighted, scale) : null;
  if (value === null || !s.newestAt) return { status: "insufficient", ...base };
  return { status: "ok", value, ...base, newestAt: s.newestAt, totalWeight: s.weighted.reduce((a, b) => a + b.weight, 0) };
}

export function vibeConsensus(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<Vibe>[], now: Date): Consensus<Vibe> {
  return categorical(reports, now, VIBE_SCALE);
}

export function lineConsensus(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<LineWait>[], now: Date): Consensus<LineWait> {
  return categorical(reports, now, LINE_SCALE);
}

export function coverConsensus(reports: readonly ConsensusReport<number>[], now: Date): Consensus<number> {
  const s = summarize(reports, now);
  const base = { reportCount: s.live.length, userCount: s.counted.length, newestAt: s.newestAt };
  const value = s.enough ? weightedMedian(s.weighted) : null;
  if (value === null || !s.newestAt) return { status: "insufficient", ...base };
  return { status: "ok", value, ...base, newestAt: s.newestAt, totalWeight: s.weighted.reduce((a, b) => a + b.weight, 0) };
}

/** Users and venue disagree on cover when the consensus differs from the official cover by more than this. */
export const COVER_MISMATCH_TOLERANCE_CENTS = 200;

export function coverMismatch(officialCents: number | null, consensus: Consensus<number>): boolean {
  return officialCents !== null && consensus.status === "ok" && Math.abs(consensus.value - officialCents) > COVER_MISMATCH_TOLERANCE_CENTS;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Reputation
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** How long after a report we wait for the consensus "around it" to form before judging it. */
export const REPUTATION_SETTLE_MS = 30 * 60_000;
/** Shrinkage: with k=4, a user needs a real track record to move far from 0.5. */
export const REPUTATION_PRIOR_K = 4;

export type Verdict = "agree" | "disagree" | "neutral";

/**
 * Reputation from a track record. s = (agree - disagree) / (agree + disagree + k) lies in (-1, 1).
 * s >= 0 moves from 0.5 toward 1.5; s < 0 moves from 0.5 toward 0.2. No history gives exactly 0.5.
 */
export function reputationFromRecord(agree: number, disagree: number): number {
  const s = (agree - disagree) / (agree + disagree + REPUTATION_PRIOR_K);
  const r = s >= 0 ? REPUTATION_NEW + s * (REPUTATION_MAX - REPUTATION_NEW) : REPUTATION_NEW + s * (REPUTATION_NEW - REPUTATION_MIN);
  return clampReputation(r);
}

/** Ordinal agreement for vibe/line: same bucket agrees, a neighbouring bucket is neutral, and further apart disagrees. */
export function ordinalVerdict<T>(scale: readonly T[], mine: T, consensus: T): Verdict {
  const d = Math.abs(scale.indexOf(mine) - scale.indexOf(consensus));
  return d === 0 ? "agree" : d === 1 ? "neutral" : "disagree";
}

/** Cover agreement: within $2 agrees, more than $5 off disagrees, and anything between is neutral. */
export function coverVerdict(mine: number, consensus: number): Verdict {
  const d = Math.abs(mine - consensus);
  return d <= 200 ? "agree" : d > 500 ? "disagree" : "neutral";
}

/**
 * Judges one report against the consensus that formed *after* it: other users' reports made in
 * (t, t + 30 min], evaluated at t + 30 min. Copying a consensus that was already on screen therefore earns
 * nothing unless later, independent reporters confirm it.
 * Returns null when those later peers don't meet the data floor (no verdict, so it doesn't count either way).
 */
export function judgeReport<T>(
  kind: "VIBE" | "LINE" | "COVER",
  report: ConsensusReport<T>,
  others: readonly ConsensusReport<T>[],
): Verdict | null {
  const t = report.createdAt.getTime();
  const at = new Date(t + REPUTATION_SETTLE_MS);
  const peers = others.filter((o) => o.userId !== report.userId && o.createdAt.getTime() > t);
  if (kind === "COVER") {
    const c = coverConsensus(peers as ConsensusReport<number>[], at);
    return c.status === "ok" ? coverVerdict(report.value as number, c.value) : null;
  }
  const scale = (kind === "VIBE" ? VIBE_SCALE : LINE_SCALE) as readonly T[];
  const c = categorical(peers, at, scale);
  return c.status === "ok" ? ordinalVerdict(scale, report.value, c.value) : null;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/accuracy/index.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Venue accuracy score. Pure: no DB, no clock.
 *
 * For each completed clock-hour window fully inside the last 30 days, a window qualifies when it holds cover
 * reports from >= 3 distinct users. Only each user's newest report in the window counts, so 3 distinct users is
 * the binding condition and also implies >= 3 reports. The window's user consensus is the reputation-weighted
 * median of those reports (the same median as the live consensus, without time decay).
 * The score only starts counting once the venue has stated a cover. Windows that end at or before the first
 * non-null official cover in the history are skipped, and a window straddling that moment is judged only on
 * the part after it. A venue that has never stated a cover gets status "no_official_cover", not a score.
 * From the first stated cover on, a window matches only when *every* official cover in effect during
 * that (part of the) hour is within $2 of the consensus. An unstated (null) or missing official cover never
 * matches. So a venue can't dodge the score: clearing its cover or changing it mid-hour can only count
 * against it, never remove the window.
 * Score = round(100 x matching / qualifying). Below 5 qualifying windows the score is "not enough data".
 *
 * Hour windows are aligned to UTC hours. America/New_York offsets are whole hours, so these are
 * also local clock hours.
 */
import { clampReputation, weightedMedian } from "@/lib/consensus";

export const ACCURACY_LOOKBACK_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000;
export const ACCURACY_WINDOW_MS = 60 * 60_000;
export const ACCURACY_MIN_USERS = 3;
export const ACCURACY_MIN_REPORTS = 3;
export const ACCURACY_TOLERANCE_CENTS = 200;
export const ACCURACY_MIN_WINDOWS = 5;

export type CoverReportInput = { userId: string; coverCents: number; createdAt: Date; reputation: number };
export type CoverHistoryInput = { coverCents: number | null; effectiveFrom: Date };

export type AccuracyWindow = { start: Date; officialCents: (number | null)[]; consensusCents: number; users: number; match: boolean };

export type AccuracyResult =
  | { status: "ok"; scorePct: number; qualifyingWindows: number; matchingWindows: number; windows: AccuracyWindow[] }
  | { status: "insufficient"; qualifyingWindows: number; matchingWindows: number; windows: AccuracyWindow[] }
  | { status: "no_official_cover"; qualifyingWindows: 0; matchingWindows: 0; windows: [] };

/** When the venue first stated a (non-null) official cover, or null if it never has. */
export function firstStatedCoverAt(history: readonly CoverHistoryInput[]): number | null {
  let first: number | null = null;
  for (const h of history) {
    if (h.coverCents === null) continue;
    const ts = h.effectiveFrom.getTime();
    if (first === null || ts < first) first = ts;
  }
  return first;
}

/** Official cover in effect at instant t (the latest history row with effectiveFrom <= t). undefined = none yet. */
export function officialCoverAt(history: readonly CoverHistoryInput[], t: number): number | null | undefined {
  let best: CoverHistoryInput | undefined;
  for (const h of history) {
    const ts = h.effectiveFrom.getTime();
    if (ts <= t && (!best || ts >= best.effectiveFrom.getTime())) best = h;
  }
  return best ? best.coverCents : undefined;
}

/** Every official cover value in effect at some point during [start, end). */
export function officialCoversDuring(history: readonly CoverHistoryInput[], start: number, end: number): (number | null)[] {
  const values: (number | null)[] = [];
  const atStart = officialCoverAt(history, start);
  if (atStart !== undefined) values.push(atStart);
  for (const h of history) {
    const ts = h.effectiveFrom.getTime();
    if (ts > start && ts < end) values.push(h.coverCents);
  }
  return values;
}

export function computeAccuracy(input: {
  reports: readonly CoverReportInput[];
  history: readonly CoverHistoryInput[];
  now: Date;
}): AccuracyResult {
  const now = input.now.getTime();
  const since = now - ACCURACY_LOOKBACK_MS;
  const firstStated = firstStatedCoverAt(input.history);
  if (firstStated === null) return { status: "no_official_cover", qualifyingWindows: 0, matchingWindows: 0, windows: [] };

  // Bucket reports by clock hour; keep the newest report per user per bucket. Only completed hours that
  // start inside the lookback count, so a window can't qualify and then flip while the hour is still running.
  const buckets = new Map<number, Map<string, CoverReportInput>>();
  for (const r of input.reports) {
    const t = r.createdAt.getTime();
    const start = Math.floor(t / ACCURACY_WINDOW_MS) * ACCURACY_WINDOW_MS;
    if (start < since || start + ACCURACY_WINDOW_MS > now) continue;
    const bucket = buckets.get(start) ?? new Map<string, CoverReportInput>();
    const prev = bucket.get(r.userId);
    if (!prev || t >= prev.createdAt.getTime()) bucket.set(r.userId, r);
    buckets.set(start, bucket);
  }

  const windows: AccuracyWindow[] = [];
  for (const [start, byUser] of [...buckets.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {
    if (byUser.size < ACCURACY_MIN_USERS || byUser.size < ACCURACY_MIN_REPORTS) continue;
    const end = start + ACCURACY_WINDOW_MS;
    if (end <= firstStated) continue; // before the venue ever posted a cover: nothing to be accurate about
    const officials = officialCoversDuring(input.history, Math.max(start, firstStated), end);
    const consensus = weightedMedian([...byUser.values()].map((r) => ({ value: r.coverCents, weight: clampReputation(r.reputation) })));
    if (consensus == null) continue;
    windows.push({
      start: new Date(start),
      officialCents: officials,
      consensusCents: consensus,
      users: byUser.size,
      match: officials.length > 0 && officials.every((o) => o != null && Math.abs(consensus - o) <= ACCURACY_TOLERANCE_CENTS),
    });
  }

  const matching = windows.filter((w) => w.match).length;
  if (windows.length < ACCURACY_MIN_WINDOWS) {
    return { status: "insufficient", qualifyingWindows: windows.length, matchingWindows: matching, windows };
  }
  return {
    status: "ok",
    scorePct: Math.round((100 * matching) / windows.length),
    qualifyingWindows: windows.length,
    matchingWindows: matching,
    windows,
  };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/reputation.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Reputation job. Runs on a schedule (every 10 minutes from src/instrumentation.ts, plus
 * `pnpm job:reputation` and the seed). It does not run on read. See DECISIONS.md, "Reputation".
 *
 * Each run judges every report from the last 90 days that is at least 30 minutes old against the
 * consensus that formed *after* it (see judgeReport). A user gets at most one verdict per venue, metric
 * and clock hour, so re-reporting every 15 minutes can't farm agreement. The job tallies agree/disagree
 * per user and writes reputationFromRecord(agree, disagree). Users with no verdicts in the lookback keep
 * their current reputation, so a pause doesn't reset a troll to newcomer weight.
 * Peers are weighted by their *current* reputation, so one run is one step of a fixed-point iteration.
 * Hidden (moderated) reports are ignored on both sides.
 *
 * Reads and computation happen outside any transaction. The write is one short transaction that takes a
 * transaction-scoped advisory lock (so concurrent runs never interleave writes) and issues a single
 * batched UPDATE.
 */
import type { ReportType } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { judgeReport, MAX_AGE_MS, REPUTATION_SETTLE_MS, reputationFromRecord, type ConsensusReport, type Verdict } from "@/lib/consensus";
import { DAY_MS, HOUR_MS } from "@/lib/time";

export const REPUTATION_LOOKBACK_MS = 90 * DAY_MS;
const JOB_LOCK_KEY = 815_2026;

type Row = { userId: string; venueId: string; type: ReportType; value: string | number; createdAt: Date };

export async function recomputeAllReputations(db: Db, now = new Date()): Promise<{ updated: number; judged: number; skipped: boolean }> {
  const { next, judged } = await computeReputations(db, now);
  const ids = [...next.keys()];
  const reps = ids.map((id) => next.get(id)!);
  if (!ids.length) return { updated: 0, judged, skipped: false };
  const updated = await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    const [row] = await tx.$queryRaw<{ locked: boolean }[]>`SELECT pg_try_advisory_xact_lock(${JOB_LOCK_KEY}) AS locked`;
    if (!row?.locked) return null; // another run holds the lock
    return tx.$executeRaw`
      UPDATE app_user AS u SET reputation = v.rep, reputation_updated_at = ${now}
      FROM (SELECT unnest(${ids}::text[]) AS id, unnest(${reps}::float8[]) AS rep) AS v
      WHERE u.id = v.id AND u.reputation IS DISTINCT FROM v.rep`;
  });
  return updated === null ? { updated: 0, judged, skipped: true } : { updated, judged, skipped: false };
}

/** Pure-ish computation (reads only): new reputation for every user who has at least one verdict. */
export async function computeReputations(db: Db, now: Date) {
  const since = new Date(now.getTime() - REPUTATION_LOOKBACK_MS);
  const [raw, users] = await Promise.all([
    db.report.findMany({
      // Exempt (App Review / QA) accounts' reports never count, on either side of a verdict.
      where: { hiddenAt: null, countable: true, createdAt: { gt: since, lte: now } },
      select: { userId: true, venueId: true, type: true, vibe: true, line: true, coverCents: true, createdAt: true },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
    }),
    db.user.findMany({ select: { id: true, reputation: true } }),
  ]);
  const rep = new Map(users.map((u) => [u.id, u.reputation]));
  const toInput = (x: Row): ConsensusReport<string | number> => ({
    userId: x.userId, value: x.value, createdAt: x.createdAt, reputation: rep.get(x.userId) ?? 0.5,
  });

  const groups = new Map<string, Row[]>();
  for (const r of raw) {
    const value = r.type === "VIBE" ? r.vibe : r.type === "LINE" ? r.line : r.coverCents;
    if (value == null) continue;
    const key = `${r.venueId}:${r.type}`;
    const list = groups.get(key) ?? [];
    list.push({ userId: r.userId, venueId: r.venueId, type: r.type, value, createdAt: r.createdAt });
    groups.set(key, list);
  }

  const judgeUntil = now.getTime() - REPUTATION_SETTLE_MS;
  // One verdict per user / venue / metric / clock hour: the first judged report in that hour.
  const verdicts = new Map<string, { userId: string; v: Verdict }>();
  let judged = 0;
  for (const rows of groups.values()) {
    let lo = 0;
    let hi = 0;
    for (const r of rows) {
      const t = r.createdAt.getTime();
      if (t > judgeUntil) break;
      // peers in the live window at t+30min: (t + 30min - 3h, t + 30min]
      while (lo < rows.length && rows[lo]!.createdAt.getTime() <= t + REPUTATION_SETTLE_MS - MAX_AGE_MS) lo++;
      while (hi < rows.length && rows[hi]!.createdAt.getTime() <= t + REPUTATION_SETTLE_MS) hi++;
      const slot = `${r.userId}:${r.venueId}:${r.type}:${Math.floor(t / HOUR_MS)}`;
      if (verdicts.has(slot)) continue;
      const v = judgeReport(r.type, toInput(r), rows.slice(lo, hi).map(toInput));
      if (!v) continue;
      judged++;
      verdicts.set(slot, { userId: r.userId, v });
    }
  }

  const tally = new Map<string, { agree: number; disagree: number }>();
  for (const { userId, v } of verdicts.values()) {
    const t = tally.get(userId) ?? { agree: 0, disagree: 0 };
    if (v === "agree") t.agree++;
    else if (v === "disagree") t.disagree++;
    tally.set(userId, t);
  }
  const next = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const [userId, t] of tally) next.set(userId, Math.round(reputationFromRecord(t.agree, t.disagree) * 1000) / 1000);
  return { next, judged };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/time.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Time conventions: everything is stored in UTC. "Tonight" is computed in America/New_York,
 * where a night runs from 5:00am local to 4:59:59.999am local the next day. A night is named by the
 * local calendar date it starts on, e.g. Friday night 11pm and Saturday 2am are both night "YYYY-MM-DD" of Friday.
 */
export const NIGHT_TZ = "America/New_York";
export const NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR = 5;

export const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
export const HOUR_MS = 60 * MINUTE_MS;
export const DAY_MS = 24 * HOUR_MS;

type LocalParts = { year: number; month: number; day: number; hour: number; minute: number; second: number };

const partsFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
  timeZone: NIGHT_TZ,
  hourCycle: "h23",
  year: "numeric",
  month: "2-digit",
  day: "2-digit",
  hour: "2-digit",
  minute: "2-digit",
  second: "2-digit",
});

/** Wall-clock components of an instant in America/New_York. */
export function localParts(date: Date): LocalParts {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const p of partsFormatter.formatToParts(date)) {
    if (p.type !== "literal") out[p.type] = Number(p.value);
  }
  return {
    year: out.year!,
    month: out.month!,
    day: out.day!,
    hour: out.hour!,
    minute: out.minute!,
    second: out.second!,
  };
}

function pad(n: number) {
  return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
}

/** The night an instant belongs to, as YYYY-MM-DD (local date the night started on). */
export function nightKey(date: Date): string {
  const p = localParts(date);
  // Use a UTC-date object purely for calendar arithmetic on the local wall date.
  const cal = new Date(Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day));
  if (p.hour < NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR) cal.setUTCDate(cal.getUTCDate() - 1);
  return `${cal.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad(cal.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad(cal.getUTCDate())}`;
}

/** Calendar arithmetic on a night key (YYYY-MM-DD), independent of daylight saving time. */
export function addNights(key: string, days: number): string {
  const [y, m, d] = key.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  const t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + days));
  return `${t.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(t.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(t.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/** Offset of America/New_York from UTC at an instant, in ms (e.g. -4h during EDT). */
function tzOffsetMs(date: Date): number {
  const p = localParts(date);
  const asUtc = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day, p.hour, p.minute, p.second);
  return asUtc - Math.floor(date.getTime() / 1000) * 1000;
}

/** UTC instant of a local America/New_York wall-clock time (resolves DST gaps forward). */
export function zonedTimeToUtc(year: number, month: number, day: number, hour: number, minute = 0): Date {
  const guess = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, hour, minute);
  // Two passes handle the DST boundary correctly.
  let ts = guess - tzOffsetMs(new Date(guess));
  ts = guess - tzOffsetMs(new Date(ts));
  return new Date(ts);
}

/** [start, end) of a night in UTC. */
export function nightBounds(key: string): { start: Date; end: Date } {
  const [y, m, d] = key.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  const start = zonedTimeToUtc(y, m, d, NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR);
  const next = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + 1));
  const end = zonedTimeToUtc(next.getUTCFullYear(), next.getUTCMonth() + 1, next.getUTCDate(), NIGHT_ROLLOVER_HOUR);
  return { start, end };
}

/** Human-friendly "12 min ago" style age. */
export function formatAge(from: Date, now: Date): string {
  const mins = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now.getTime() - from.getTime()) / MINUTE_MS));
  if (mins < 1) return "just now";
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins} min ago`;
  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  if (hours < 24) return `${hours} hr ago`;
  return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)} d ago`;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/geo.ts
====================================================================================================

export const GEOFENCE_METERS = 100;
const EARTH_RADIUS_M = 6_371_008.8;

export type LatLng = { lat: number; lng: number };

/** Great-circle distance in meters (haversine). */
export function distanceMeters(a: LatLng, b: LatLng): number {
  const toRad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLat = toRad(b.lat - a.lat);
  const dLng = toRad(b.lng - a.lng);
  const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(toRad(a.lat)) * Math.cos(toRad(b.lat)) * Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_M * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h)));
}

export function isValidLatLng(p: { lat: unknown; lng: unknown }): p is LatLng {
  return (
    typeof p.lat === "number" &&
    typeof p.lng === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(p.lat) &&
    Number.isFinite(p.lng) &&
    Math.abs(p.lat) <= 90 &&
    Math.abs(p.lng) <= 180
  );
}

/** Inclusive: exactly 100m away counts as inside. */
export function withinGeofence(user: LatLng, venue: LatLng, radius = GEOFENCE_METERS): boolean {
  return distanceMeters(user, venue) <= radius;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/permissions.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Central permission checks for the service layer. Every mutating service calls one of these before touching
 * the database; the UI hiding a button is never the enforcement point.
 *
 * Checks and balances:
 *  - Venues own the facts: only an approved owner of a venue may edit its official info / promos / replies.
 *  - Users own the reality: only the author may edit their own comment; nobody may delete user content;
 *    only MODERATOR/ADMIN may hide or restore it. A VENUE_OWNER gets 403 on any attempt to modify user
 *    content (invariant 1), and cannot post "user reality" (reports, photos, top-level comments) for a
 *    venue they own.
 */
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { forbidden, unauthorized } from "@/server/errors";

export function requireSignedIn(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  if (!actor) throw unauthorized();
  return actor;
}

export function isModerator(actor: Actor | null): boolean {
  return actor?.role === "MODERATOR" || actor?.role === "ADMIN";
}

export function ownsVenue(actor: Actor | null, venueId: string): boolean {
  return Boolean(actor && actor.role === "VENUE_OWNER" && actor.ownedVenueIds.includes(venueId));
}

export function assertOwnsVenue(actor: Actor | null, venueId: string): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (!ownsVenue(a, venueId)) throw forbidden("Only an approved owner of this venue can do that");
  return a;
}

export function assertModerator(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (!isModerator(a)) throw forbidden("Moderators only");
  return a;
}

export function assertAdmin(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (a.role !== "ADMIN") throw forbidden("Admins only");
  return a;
}

/** Users own the reality: a venue's own account cannot post reports/photos/top-level comments for it. */
export function assertCanPostUserContent(actor: Actor | null, venueId: string): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (ownsVenue(a, venueId)) {
    throw forbidden("Venue accounts can't post live reports, photos or comments for their own venue. Reply to comments instead.");
  }
  return a;
}

/**
 * Editing user-generated content: author only. Venue owners get an explicit 403 even for content at their venue;
 * moderators don't edit (they hide), so they're refused too.
 */
export function assertCanEditOwnContent(actor: Actor | null, content: { userId: string; venueId: string }): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (a.userId !== content.userId) {
    throw forbidden(ownsVenue(a, content.venueId) ? "Venues can't edit user content. Flag it for a moderator instead." : "You can only edit your own content");
  }
  return a;
}

/** Hiding / restoring user content is a moderator decision (invariant 1: never the venue's). */
export function assertCanModerateContent(actor: Actor | null): Actor {
  const a = requireSignedIn(actor);
  if (!isModerator(a)) {
    throw forbidden(a.role === "VENUE_OWNER" ? "Venues can't hide user content. Flag it and a moderator will review." : "Moderators only");
  }
  return a;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/actor.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Role, SessionClient } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { getSessionUser } from "@/lib/auth/session";

/** The authenticated caller, as seen by the service layer. Every permission check takes one of these. */
export type Actor = {
  userId: string;
  role: Role;
  handle: string;
  displayName: string;
  /** Venues with an APPROVED claim. Only meaningful for VENUE_OWNER. */
  ownedVenueIds: string[];
  /** App Review / QA account: may post from anywhere; its posts never count (see DECISIONS.md). */
  geofenceExempt: boolean;
};

export async function loadActor(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<Actor | null> {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: userId },
    include: { claims: { where: { status: "APPROVED" }, select: { venueId: true } } },
  });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || user.suspendedAt) return null;
  return {
    userId: user.id,
    role: user.role,
    handle: user.handle,
    displayName: user.displayName,
    ownedVenueIds: user.role === "VENUE_OWNER" ? user.claims.map((c) => c.venueId) : [],
    geofenceExempt: user.geofenceExempt,
  };
}

export async function actorFromToken(db: Db, token: string | undefined | null, client?: SessionClient): Promise<Actor | null> {
  const user = await getSessionUser(db, token, { client });
  return user ? loadActor(db, user.id) : null;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/http.ts
====================================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { ZodError, type ZodType } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { actorFromToken, type Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { ServiceError, badRequest, forbidden, unauthorized } from "@/server/errors";

/** Reads one cookie from a raw Request (works in route handlers and in tests without Next's request scope). */
export function readCookie(req: Request, name: string): string | undefined {
  const header = req.headers.get("cookie");
  if (!header) return undefined;
  for (const part of header.split(";")) {
    const idx = part.indexOf("=");
    if (idx === -1) continue;
    if (part.slice(0, idx).trim() === name) return decodeURIComponent(part.slice(idx + 1).trim());
  }
  return undefined;
}

/**
 * Client IP for rate limiting: first hop of X-Forwarded-For (on Vercel that is the real client, set by the
 * edge), then X-Real-IP, else "unknown". Never used for authorization.
 */
export function clientIp(req: Request): string {
  const xff = req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for");
  const first = xff?.split(",")[0]?.trim();
  return (first || req.headers.get("x-real-ip")?.trim() || "unknown").slice(0, 64);
}

/**
 * How a request authenticates. If an Authorization header is present it is the only credential considered
 * (a malformed or unknown bearer token is unauthenticated, never a fallback to the cookie).
 */
export function credentials(req: Request): { kind: "bearer" | "cookie" | "none"; token: string | null } {
  const auth = req.headers.get("authorization");
  if (auth !== null) {
    // Auth scheme is case-insensitive (RFC 7235); tokens are base64url.
    const m = /^bearer\s+([A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,200})$/i.exec(auth.trim());
    return { kind: "bearer", token: m ? m[1]! : null };
  }
  const cookie = readCookie(req, SESSION_COOKIE);
  return cookie ? { kind: "cookie", token: cookie } : { kind: "none", token: null };
}

/**
 * The caller, or null when anonymous. A request that *sends* a bearer token which doesn't resolve (malformed,
 * expired, revoked, or not an iOS session) gets 401 `invalid_token` on every route, public ones included,
 * so the app learns immediately that it must sign in again instead of silently seeing anonymous data.
 */
export async function getActor(req: Request): Promise<Actor | null> {
  const cred = credentials(req);
  if (cred.kind === "bearer") {
    const actor = cred.token ? await actorFromToken(prisma, cred.token, "IOS") : null;
    if (!actor) throw new ServiceError(401, "invalid_token", "Your session has expired. Sign in again.");
    return actor;
  }
  return cred.token ? actorFromToken(prisma, cred.token, "WEB") : null;
}

export async function requireActor(req: Request): Promise<Actor> {
  const actor = await getActor(req);
  if (!actor) throw unauthorized();
  return actor;
}

/**
 * CSRF defense for cookie-authenticated mutations: SameSite=Lax cookies plus an Origin check.
 * Browsers always send Origin on cross-site POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE, so a mismatch is rejected.
 * Bearer-authenticated requests (the iOS app) are exempt: a cross-site page can't attach an Authorization
 * header without a CORS preflight, which this API never grants, and the cookie is ignored when a bearer
 * header is present.
 */
export function assertSameOrigin(req: Request) {
  if (req.method === "GET" || req.method === "HEAD") return;
  if (credentials(req).kind === "bearer") return;
  const origin = req.headers.get("origin");
  if (!origin) return; // non-browser clients (tests, curl) don't send Origin; cookies are still SameSite=Lax
  const host = req.headers.get("x-forwarded-host") ?? req.headers.get("host");
  let originHost: string;
  try {
    originHost = new URL(origin).host;
  } catch {
    throw forbidden("Bad origin");
  }
  if (!host || originHost !== host) throw forbidden("Cross-origin request rejected");
}

/** Reads a JSON body without validating it; the service layer owns validation (and may transform values). */
export async function readJson(req: Request): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await req.json();
  } catch {
    throw badRequest("Body must be JSON");
  }
}

export async function parseJson<T>(req: Request, schema: ZodType<T>): Promise<T> {
  let raw: unknown;
  try {
    raw = await req.json();
  } catch {
    throw badRequest("Body must be JSON");
  }
  return schema.parse(raw);
}

type Handler<C> = (req: Request, ctx: C) => Promise<Response>;

/** Wraps a route handler: same-origin check for mutations and uniform error mapping. */
export function route<C>(handler: Handler<C>): Handler<C> {
  return async (req, ctx) => {
    try {
      assertSameOrigin(req);
      return await handler(req, ctx);
    } catch (err) {
      return errorResponse(err);
    }
  };
}

export function errorResponse(err: unknown): Response {
  if (err instanceof ServiceError) {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (err.code === "invalid_token") headers["www-authenticate"] = 'Bearer error="invalid_token"';
    else if (err.status === 401) headers["www-authenticate"] = 'Bearer realm="lastcall"';
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.code, message: err.message }, { status: err.status, headers });
  }
  if (err instanceof ZodError) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "invalid_input", message: err.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join(".") || "body"}: ${i.message}`).join("; ") },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }
  // A database backstop fired (the service checks first, so this is a race at a boundary, e.g. a rating edit that
  // commits just after 5am). The triggers' messages start with "lastcall:"; answer 409 with that sentence.
  const guard = err instanceof Error ? /lastcall: ([^\n"]+)/.exec(err.message) : null;
  if (guard) {
    const text = guard[1]!.trim();
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "conflict", message: text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1) }, { status: 409 });
  }
  console.error("[api] unhandled error", err);
  return NextResponse.json({ error: "internal", message: "Something went wrong" }, { status: 500 });
}

export const ok = (data: unknown, status = 200) => NextResponse.json(data, { status });


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/reports.ts
