  const rows = await db.block.findMany({
    where: { blockerId: me.userId, blocked: { deletedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: { ref: true, source: true, createdAt: true, blocked: { select: { handle: true } } },
  });
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    ref: r.ref,
    source: r.source as "profile" | "photo" | "comment",
    handle: r.source === "profile" ? r.blocked.handle : null,
    blockedAt: r.createdAt,
  }));
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/me.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION } from "@/server/services/auth";
import { avatarUrl } from "@/server/services/people";

/** The signed-in account as the app needs it at launch: identity, role, owned venues and settings. */
export async function getMe(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: me.userId },
    select: {
      id: true, email: true, handle: true, displayName: true, role: true, createdAt: true, reputation: true,
      termsAcceptedAt: true, termsVersion: true, geofenceExempt: true, isPrivate: true, bio: true, avatarKey: true, verifiedAt: true,
    },
  });
  if (!user) throw notFound("Account not found");
  const pendingFollowRequests = await db.followRequest.count({ where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } });
  const venues = me.ownedVenueIds.length
    ? await db.venue.findMany({ where: { id: { in: me.ownedVenueIds } }, orderBy: { name: "asc" }, select: { id: true, slug: true, name: true } })
    : [];
  return {
    user: {
      id: user.id, email: user.email, handle: user.handle, displayName: user.displayName, role: user.role,
      memberSince: user.createdAt,
      /** Private: returned only to the account itself (and moderators on profiles). */
      reputation: user.reputation,
      isPrivate: user.isPrivate,
      bio: user.bio,
      avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(user.avatarKey, now),
      verified: user.verifiedAt !== null,
    },
    social: { pendingFollowRequests },
    ownedVenues: venues,
    /** Read-only account settings the app displays. None are user-editable yet. */
    settings: {
      /** Going-tonight plans are shared with mutual follows only (see DECISIONS.md). */
      plansVisibleTo: "mutuals" as const,
      /** App Review / QA account: can post anywhere, but posts don't count. Set by an admin only. */
      geofenceExempt: user.geofenceExempt,
    },
    terms: {
      acceptedVersion: user.termsVersion,
      acceptedAt: user.termsAcceptedAt,
      currentVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION,
      /** True when the app should ask the user to accept the current Terms and Privacy Policy. */
      needsAcceptance: user.termsVersion !== CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION,
    },
  };
}

/** Records acceptance of the current Terms/Privacy (when /api/me says needsAcceptance). */
export async function acceptCurrentTerms(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const body = (raw ?? {}) as { version?: unknown };
  if (body.version !== CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION) throw badRequest(`Accept the current terms version (${CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION})`);
  await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { termsVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION, termsAcceptedAt: now } });
  return { acceptedVersion: CURRENT_TERMS_VERSION, acceptedAt: now };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/privacy.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Who can see what (the social layer's privacy rules). Every payload that names a person goes through this file.
 *
 * Accounts are private by default. A viewer sees a person's **full** profile (display name, photo, bio, counts,
 * posts, Regular badges, featured history) when any of these hold:
 *  - the viewer is that person, or a moderator/admin (staff always see real people);
 *  - the account is public;
 *  - the viewer is an approved follower (a pending request is not enough).
 * Otherwise the viewer gets the **limited** view: the handle, the verified badge and a generic avatar only.
 *
 * Authors on venue pages (photos, comments, replies, featured photos) are resolved per viewer: the handle (with
 * the verified badge and the Regular badge) only when the viewer may see the full profile, otherwise
 * "Anonymous" with no handle and no link. A private author's handle never reaches a viewer who can't see it.
 * Deleted accounts are "Deleted user" for everyone.
 *
 * Blocks (either direction) override everything except staff: a blocked pair gets the limited view, "Anonymous",
 * and no photos. Callers also filter blocked people out (and 404 their profiles); these rules are the backstop
 * so a forgotten caller check can't leak.
 *
 * Suspended accounts are limited/"Anonymous" to everyone but staff (their profile 404s, so a handle would lead
 * nowhere), and callers' lists and search skip them.
 *
 * The functions here are pure; `followedAmong` and `blockedAmong` load the relations they need.
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";

/** The viewer as the rules need it. null = signed out. */
export type Viewer = { userId: string; staff: boolean } | null;

/** The person being shown. */
export type Subject = { id: string; isPrivate: boolean; deleted: boolean; suspended?: boolean };

export type Access = "full" | "limited";

export function viewerOf(actor: Actor | null): Viewer {
  return actor ? { userId: actor.userId, staff: actor.role === "MODERATOR" || actor.role === "ADMIN" } : null;
}

/**
 * Full or limited view of a person. `viewerFollows` is an APPROVED follow from the viewer to the subject; `blocked`
 * is a block in either direction. Deleted accounts are always limited (callers 404 them or show "Deleted user").
 */
export function profileAccess(viewer: Viewer, subject: Subject, viewerFollows: boolean, blocked = false): Access {
  if (subject.deleted) return "limited";
  if (viewer && (viewer.userId === subject.id || viewer.staff)) return "full";
  if (blocked || subject.suspended) return "limited";
  if (!subject.isPrivate) return "full";
  return viewer && viewerFollows ? "full" : "limited";
}

export type AuthorView =
  | { kind: "user"; handle: string; verified: boolean; regular: boolean }
  | { kind: "anonymous" }
  | { kind: "deleted" };

export type AuthorSubject = Subject & { handle: string; verified: boolean };

/**
 * Author of a photo, comment or reply as this viewer may see it. `regular` is the author's Regular status at the
 * venue being shown (false outside a venue context); it's only ever attached to a visible handle.
 */
export function resolveAuthor(viewer: Viewer, author: AuthorSubject, viewerFollows: boolean, regular = false, blocked = false): AuthorView {
  if (author.deleted) return { kind: "deleted" };
  if (profileAccess(viewer, author, viewerFollows, blocked) === "limited") return { kind: "anonymous" };
  return { kind: "user", handle: author.handle, verified: author.verified, regular };
}

/** A person in a list (search, followers, requests, activity). The handle and badge always; the rest when allowed. */
export type PersonView = { handle: string; displayName: string | null; avatarUrl: string | null; verified: boolean };

export function personView(
  access: Access,
  person: { handle: string; displayName: string; verified: boolean },
  avatarUrl: string | null,
): PersonView {
  return access === "full"
    ? { handle: person.handle, displayName: person.displayName, avatarUrl, verified: person.verified }
    : { handle: person.handle, displayName: null, avatarUrl: null, verified: person.verified };
}

/** What a photo is, for visibility decisions. */
export type PhotoSubject = {
  authorId: string;
  /** null = live-only photo (always shown on the venue page, never on the profile). */
  rating: number | null;
  shared: boolean;
  hidden: boolean;
  removed: boolean;
  /** false = posted by a geofence-exempt (App Review) account: visible to its author only. */
  countable: boolean;
};

/** Whether the photo appears in its venue's feed (live, earlier and featured) for this viewer, before blocks. */
export function inVenueFeed(viewer: Viewer, photo: PhotoSubject): boolean {
  if (photo.hidden || photo.removed) return false;
  if (!photo.countable && viewer?.userId !== photo.authorId) return false;
  return photo.rating === null || photo.shared;
}

/**
 * Whether the viewer may open this post (post detail). Posts only: a live-only photo is reachable through the
 * venue feed's time window, never by id. Shared posts are public, with the author resolved per viewer; unshared
 * posts need the full profile. Hidden and removed posts are for staff only (so a flagged post an author removes
 * can still be moderated). A block in either direction hides it from the other side.
 */
export function canSeePhoto(viewer: Viewer, photo: PhotoSubject, authorAccess: Access, blocked = false): boolean {
  if (viewer?.staff) return true;
  if (photo.rating === null || photo.removed || photo.hidden) return false;
  if (viewer?.userId === photo.authorId) return true;
  if (blocked || !photo.countable) return false;
  if (photo.shared) return true;
  return authorAccess === "full";
}

/** Ids among `ids` that the viewer follows (approved follows only). */
export async function followedAmong(db: Db, viewerId: string | null | undefined, ids: readonly string[]): Promise<Set<string>> {
  if (!viewerId || ids.length === 0) return new Set();
  const rows = await db.follow.findMany({
    where: { followerId: viewerId, followeeId: { in: [...new Set(ids)] } },
    select: { followeeId: true },
  });
  return new Set(rows.map((r) => r.followeeId));
}

/** Ids among `ids` that have a block with the viewer, in either direction. */
export async function blockedAmong(db: Db, viewerId: string | null | undefined, ids: readonly string[]): Promise<Set<string>> {
  if (!viewerId || ids.length === 0) return new Set();
  const unique = [...new Set(ids)];
  const rows = await db.block.findMany({
    where: { OR: [{ blockerId: viewerId, blockedId: { in: unique } }, { blockedId: viewerId, blockerId: { in: unique } }] },
    select: { blockerId: true, blockedId: true },
  });
  return new Set(rows.map((r) => (r.blockerId === viewerId ? r.blockedId : r.blockerId)));
}

/** Resolves many people at once: loads follows and blocks once, then applies the rules to each. */
export async function authorResolver(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, authorIds: readonly string[]) {
  const viewer = viewerOf(actor);
  const [follows, blocks] = await Promise.all([followedAmong(db, viewer?.userId, authorIds), blockedAmong(db, viewer?.userId, authorIds)]);
  return {
    viewer,
    follows,
    blocks,
    access: (s: Subject) => profileAccess(viewer, s, follows.has(s.id), blocks.has(s.id)),
    author: (s: AuthorSubject, regular = false) => resolveAuthor(viewer, s, follows.has(s.id), regular, blocks.has(s.id)),
  };
}

/** Prisma select for everything the rules need about a person. */
export const subjectSelect = {
  id: true, handle: true, displayName: true, isPrivate: true, deletedAt: true, suspendedAt: true, verifiedAt: true, avatarKey: true,
} as const;

export type SubjectRow = {
  id: string; handle: string; displayName: string; isPrivate: boolean; deletedAt: Date | null; suspendedAt: Date | null; verifiedAt: Date | null; avatarKey: string | null;
};

export const toSubject = (u: SubjectRow): AuthorSubject => ({
  id: u.id, handle: u.handle, isPrivate: u.isPrivate, deleted: u.deletedAt !== null, suspended: u.suspendedAt !== null, verified: u.verifiedAt !== null,
});


====================================================================================================
== src/server/rate-limit.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Per-user rate limits for social actions (follows and requests, likes, posts). Same pattern as the report cooldown
 * and the flag limit: a transaction-scoped advisory lock on (kind, user) makes the count exact under parallel
 * requests, and works behind PgBouncer in transaction mode. Actions are counted in the `rate_event` ledger rather
 * than from the rows they create, because unfollowing and unliking delete those rows.
 */
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { HOUR_MS, DAY_MS } from "@/lib/time";
import { tooMany } from "@/server/errors";

export const RATE_LIMITS = {
  /** Follows and follow requests. */
  follow: { max: 60, windowMs: HOUR_MS, message: "You've followed a lot of people recently. Try again later." },
  like: { max: 200, windowMs: HOUR_MS, message: "You've liked a lot of posts recently. Try again later." },
  /** Across all venues (each venue also has the 15-minute photo cooldown). */
  post: { max: 20, windowMs: HOUR_MS, message: "You've posted a lot recently. Try again later." },
} as const;

export type RateKind = keyof typeof RATE_LIMITS;

/** Counts one action against the user's limit, or throws 429. Must run inside a transaction. */
export async function consumeRate(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, userId: string, kind: RateKind, now: Date) {
  const limit = RATE_LIMITS[kind];
  await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`rate:${kind}:${userId}`}))`;
  const recent = await tx.rateEvent.count({ where: { userId, kind, createdAt: { gt: new Date(now.getTime() - limit.windowMs) } } });
  if (recent >= limit.max) throw tooMany(limit.message);
  await tx.rateEvent.create({ data: { userId, kind, createdAt: now } });
}

/** Deletes ledger rows older than a day (every window is shorter). Run by the cron job. */
export async function pruneRateEvents(db: Db, now = new Date()) {
  const { count } = await db.rateEvent.deleteMany({ where: { createdAt: { lt: new Date(now.getTime() - DAY_MS) } } });
  return count;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/people.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * People: profiles, follows and follow requests, followers and following lists, search, profile settings.
 *
 * Privacy (src/server/privacy.ts): accounts are private by default. Following a public account is instant;
 * following a private one creates a request its owner approves or declines. Only approved follows exist in the
 * `follow` table, so "friends" for plans (mutual follows) are mutual *approved* follows by construction.
 * A viewer without full access to a private profile gets the handle, the verified badge and a Request button only.
 *
 * Blocking removes follows and pending requests in both directions and prevents new ones (blocks.ts).
 */
import sharp from "sharp";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { getStorage, MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, newStorageKey, sniffImage, type BlobStorage } from "@/lib/storage";
import { nightKey } from "@/lib/time";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { assertCleanText, badRequest, forbidden, notFound, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { isModerator, requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { followedAmong, personView, profileAccess, subjectSelect, toSubject, viewerOf, type PersonView, type SubjectRow } from "@/server/privacy";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";
import { blockedIdsFor, isBlockedEitherWay, lockPair } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/services/venues";
import { regularVenuesOf } from "@/server/services/regulars";
import { featuredHistory } from "@/server/services/gallery";

export type FollowState = "none" | "requested" | "following";

/** Signed URL for a profile photo, or null. */
export const avatarUrl = async (key: string | null, now = new Date()) => (key ? uploadUrl(key, now) : null);

async function activeUser(db: Db, handle: string, opts: { allowSuspended?: boolean } = {}) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !opts.allowSuspended)) throw notFound("User not found");
  return user;
}

/** The viewer's follow state toward each of `ids` (approved follows and pending requests). */
async function followStates(db: Db, viewerId: string | null | undefined, ids: readonly string[]) {
  const out = new Map<string, FollowState>();
  if (!viewerId || !ids.length) return out;
  const [follows, requests] = await Promise.all([
    followedAmong(db, viewerId, ids),
    db.followRequest.findMany({ where: { requesterId: viewerId, targetId: { in: [...ids] } }, select: { targetId: true } }),
  ]);
  for (const r of requests) out.set(r.targetId, "requested");
  for (const id of follows) out.set(id, "following");
  return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Following and requests
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Follow a public account (instant) or request to follow a private one. Idempotent. */
export async function follow(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, now = new Date()): Promise<{ state: FollowState }> {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const target = await activeUser(db, handle);
  if (target.id === me.userId) throw badRequest("You can't follow yourself");
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Locks both directions of the pair (the same locks a block takes): a double tap can't create both a follow
    // and a request, and a block can't commit between the check below and the insert.
    await lockPair(tx, me.userId, target.id);
    if (await isBlockedEitherWay(tx as Db, me.userId, target.id)) throw forbidden("You can't follow this account");
    const [existing, pending, fresh] = await Promise.all([
      tx.follow.findUnique({ where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: target.id } } }),
      tx.followRequest.findUnique({ where: { requesterId_targetId: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id } } }),
      tx.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: target.id }, select: { isPrivate: true } }),
    ]);
    if (existing) return { state: "following" as const };
    if (pending && fresh.isPrivate) return { state: "requested" as const };
    await consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "follow", now);
    if (!fresh.isPrivate) {
      await tx.follow.create({ data: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: target.id, createdAt: now } });
      await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id } });
      return { state: "following" as const };
    }
    await tx.followRequest.create({ data: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id, createdAt: now } });
    return { state: "requested" as const };
  });
}

/** Unfollow, or cancel a pending request. Idempotent. */
export async function unfollow(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string): Promise<{ state: FollowState }> {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const target = await activeUser(db, handle, { allowSuspended: true });
  // Same pair lock as follow and approve: a cancel can't lose a race against its own approval.
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockPair(tx, me.userId, target.id);
    await tx.follow.deleteMany({ where: { followerId: me.userId, followeeId: target.id } });
    await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { requesterId: me.userId, targetId: target.id } });
  });
  return { state: "none" };
}

/** Requests waiting for the signed-in user's decision, newest first. */
export async function listFollowRequests(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const rows = await db.followRequest.findMany({
    where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    take: 200,
    select: { createdAt: true, requester: { select: subjectSelect } },
  });
  const follows = await followedAmong(db, me.userId, rows.map((r) => r.requester.id));
  const viewer = viewerOf(me);
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => {
      const access = profileAccess(viewer, toSubject(r.requester), follows.has(r.requester.id));
      return {
        person: personView(access, { ...r.requester, verified: r.requester.verifiedAt !== null }, await avatarUrl(r.requester.avatarKey, now)),
        requestedAt: r.createdAt,
      };
    }),
  );
}

export const requestDecisionSchema = z.object({ approve: z.boolean() }).strict();

/** Approve or decline one request. Only requests addressed to the signed-in user can be found (404 otherwise). */
export async function decideFollowRequest(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const { approve } = requestDecisionSchema.parse(raw);
  const requester = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true, deletedAt: true, suspendedAt: true } });
  if (!requester || requester.deletedAt) throw notFound("No follow request from this account");
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await lockPair(tx, requester.id, me.userId);
    const { count } = await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { requesterId: requester.id, targetId: me.userId } });
    if (count === 0) throw notFound("No follow request from this account");
    if (!approve) return { approved: false };
    if (requester.suspendedAt || (await isBlockedEitherWay(tx as Db, me.userId, requester.id))) throw notFound("No follow request from this account");
    await tx.follow.upsert({
      where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: requester.id, followeeId: me.userId } },
      create: { followerId: requester.id, followeeId: me.userId, createdAt: now },
      update: {},
    });
    return { approved: true };
  });
}

/** Approve every pending request at once (offered after switching a private account to public). */
export async function approveAllFollowRequests(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    const pending = await tx.followRequest.findMany({ where: { targetId: me.userId }, select: { requesterId: true } });
    // Lock every pair first (the locks blocks and cancels take), then decide from what's still there.
    for (const r of [...pending].sort((a, b) => a.requesterId.localeCompare(b.requesterId))) await lockPair(tx, r.requesterId, me.userId);
    const blocked = await blockedIdsFor(tx as Db, me.userId);
    const rows = await tx.followRequest.findMany({
      where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null }, requesterId: { notIn: blocked } },
      select: { requesterId: true },
    });
    const ids = rows.map((r) => r.requesterId);
    if (!ids.length) return { approved: 0 };
    await tx.follow.createMany({ data: ids.map((id) => ({ followerId: id, followeeId: me.userId, createdAt: now })), skipDuplicates: true });
    await tx.followRequest.deleteMany({ where: { targetId: me.userId, requesterId: { in: ids } } });
    return { approved: ids.length };
  });
}

/** Remove someone who follows you. Idempotent. */
export async function removeFollower(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const other = await activeUser(db, handle, { allowSuspended: true });
  await db.follow.deleteMany({ where: { followerId: other.id, followeeId: me.userId } });
  return { removed: true as const };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Profiles and lists
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Posts on a profile: rated photos that aren't hidden or removed. App Review posts count only for their author. */
export const profilePostsWhere = (userId: string, isSelf: boolean) =>
  ({ userId, rating: { not: null }, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null, ...(isSelf ? {} : { countable: true }) }) as const;

export async function getProfile(db: Db, handle: string, viewer: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({
    where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() },
    select: {
      ...subjectSelect, role: true, createdAt: true, reputation: true, bio: true,
      claims: { where: { status: "APPROVED" }, select: { venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } } },
    },
  });
  if (!user) throw notFound("User not found");
  const isSelf = viewer?.userId === user.id;
  // Deleted accounts can't be looked up, and blocked users (either direction) don't see each other.
  if (user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(viewer))) throw notFound("User not found");
  if (viewer && !isSelf && (await isBlockedEitherWay(db, viewer.userId, user.id))) throw notFound("User not found");

  const [states, followsViewer] = await Promise.all([
    followStates(db, viewer?.userId, [user.id]),
    viewer && !isSelf
      ? db.follow.findUnique({ where: { followerId_followeeId: { followerId: user.id, followeeId: viewer.userId } } }).then(Boolean)
      : Promise.resolve(false),
  ]);
  const follow: FollowState = isSelf ? "none" : (states.get(user.id) ?? "none");
  const relationship = { isSelf, follow, followsYou: followsViewer, mutual: follow === "following" && followsViewer };
  const access = profileAccess(viewerOf(viewer), toSubject(user), follow === "following");
  const verified = user.verifiedAt !== null;

  if (access === "limited") {
    return { access: "limited" as const, user: { handle: user.handle, verified, isPrivate: true as const }, relationship };
  }

  const [followers, following, posts, goingTonight, regularAt, featured] = await Promise.all([
    // Same people the lists show (active accounts).
    db.follow.count({ where: { followeeId: user.id, follower: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
    db.follow.count({ where: { followerId: user.id, followee: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
    db.photo.count({ where: profilePostsWhere(user.id, isSelf) }),
    // Going-tonight plans: yourself and mutual follows only.
    isSelf || relationship.mutual
      ? db.rsvp.findMany({ where: { userId: user.id, night: nightKey(now) }, select: { venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } } })
      : Promise.resolve(null),
    regularVenuesOf(db, user.id, now),
    featuredHistory(db, user.id),
  ]);
  return {
    access: "full" as const,
    user: {
      handle: user.handle,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(user.avatarKey, now),
      bio: user.bio,
      verified,
      isPrivate: user.isPrivate,
      /** Venue accounts are labelled; staff roles (moderator/admin) are never revealed publicly. */
      isVenueAccount: user.role === "VENUE_OWNER",
      memberSince: user.createdAt,
      // Reputation is private: shown to the account itself and to moderators only.
      reputation: isSelf || isModerator(viewer) ? user.reputation : null,
      counts: { posts, followers, following },
      venues: user.role === "VENUE_OWNER" ? user.claims.map((c) => c.venue) : [],
    },
    relationship,
    goingTonight: goingTonight?.map((r) => r.venue) ?? null,
    /** "Regular at ___" badges (full access only, like everything else on the profile). */
    regularAt,
    /** "Featured at Lantern & Lark, October". */
    featured,
  };
}

export type ProfileView = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getProfile>>;

/** A list of people as the viewer may see them, with the viewer's follow state toward each. */
async function peopleView(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, rows: SubjectRow[], now: Date) {
  const states = await followStates(db, viewer?.userId, rows.map((r) => r.id));
  const v = viewerOf(viewer);
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async (r) => {
      const state = states.get(r.id) ?? "none";
      const access = profileAccess(v, toSubject(r), state === "following");
      return {
        ...personView(access, { ...r, verified: r.verifiedAt !== null }, access === "full" ? await avatarUrl(r.avatarKey, now) : null),
        follow: state,
        isSelf: viewer?.userId === r.id,
      };
    }),
  );
}

/** Followers or following of a profile. Needs full access to the profile (403 `private_account` otherwise). */
export async function listConnections(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, handle: string, which: "followers" | "following", now = new Date()) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(viewer))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const blocked = await blockedIdsFor(db, viewer?.userId);
  if (blocked.includes(user.id)) throw notFound("User not found");
  const follows = await followedAmong(db, viewer?.userId, [user.id]);
  if (profileAccess(viewerOf(viewer), toSubject(user), follows.has(user.id)) === "limited") {
    throw new ServiceError(403, "private_account", "This account is private. Follow it to see who they follow and who follows them.");
  }
  const active = { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null, id: { notIn: blocked } };
  const rows =
    which === "followers"
      ? (await db.follow.findMany({ where: { followeeId: user.id, follower: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 500, select: { follower: { select: subjectSelect } } })).map((r) => r.follower)
      : (await db.follow.findMany({ where: { followerId: user.id, followee: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 500, select: { followee: { select: subjectSelect } } })).map((r) => r.followee);
  return peopleView(db, viewer, rows, now);
}

/**
 * People search by handle, and by display name where the viewer may see it (public accounts, people they follow).
 * Matching a private account's display name would reveal it, so those match on the handle only.
 */
export async function searchUsers(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, q: string, now = new Date()) {
  const query = q.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^@/, "");
  if (query.length < 2) return [];
  const nameVisible = [{ isPrivate: false }, ...(viewer ? [{ followers: { some: { followerId: viewer.userId } } }] : [])];
  const users = await db.user.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [{ handle: { contains: query } }, { displayName: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" }, OR: nameVisible }],
      deletedAt: null,
      suspendedAt: null,
      ...(viewer ? { id: { notIn: [viewer.userId, ...(await blockedIdsFor(db, viewer.userId))] } } : {}),
    },
    take: 20,
    orderBy: { handle: "asc" },
    select: subjectSelect,
  });
  return peopleView(db, viewer, users, now);
}

/** People you follow, with tonight's plans for mutual follows only (see social.ts on privacy). */
export async function listFollowing(db: Db, viewer: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(viewer);
  const rows = await db.follow.findMany({
    where: { followerId: me.userId, followee: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } },
    orderBy: { followee: { handle: "asc" } },
    select: {
      followee: {
        select: {
          ...subjectSelect,
          following: { where: { followeeId: me.userId }, select: { followerId: true } },
          rsvps: { where: { night: nightKey(now) }, select: { venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } } },
        },
      },
    },
  });
  return Promise.all(
    rows.map(async ({ followee: f }) => {
      const mutual = f.following.length > 0;
      // You follow them (approved), so you have full access.
      return {
        handle: f.handle, displayName: f.displayName, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(f.avatarKey, now), verified: f.verifiedAt !== null,
        mutual, goingTonight: mutual ? f.rsvps.map((x) => x.venue) : [],
      };
    }),
  );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Profile settings
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const BIO_MAX = 150;

export const profileSettingsSchema = z
  .object({
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1, "Enter a name").max(60, "Name: 60 characters max"),
    bio: z.string().trim().max(BIO_MAX, `Bio: ${BIO_MAX} characters max`),
    isPrivate: z.boolean(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict();

async function settingsView(db: Db, userId: string, now: Date) {
  const [u, pendingRequests] = await Promise.all([
    db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: userId }, select: { handle: true, displayName: true, bio: true, isPrivate: true, avatarKey: true, verifiedAt: true } }),
    db.followRequest.count({ where: { targetId: userId, requester: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } } }),
  ]);
  return {
    profile: { handle: u.handle, displayName: u.displayName, bio: u.bio, isPrivate: u.isPrivate, avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(u.avatarKey, now), verified: u.verifiedAt !== null },
    /** Still pending after switching to public: the app offers "Approve N pending requests?". */
    pendingRequests,
  };
}

/**
 * Edit display name, bio and the private toggle. Switching private to public doesn't approve pending requests;
 * that's a separate, explicit action (approveAllFollowRequests).
 */
export async function updateProfile(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const input = profileSettingsSchema.parse(raw);
  assertCleanText({ name: input.displayName, bio: input.bio });
  await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: input });
  return settingsView(db, me.userId, now);
}

export async function getProfileSettings(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  return settingsView(db, requireSignedIn(actor).userId, now);
}

export const AVATAR_SIZE = 512;

/** Profile photos: same checks as venue photos (size, magic bytes, full decode), then a square JPEG with no metadata. */
export async function processAvatar(input: Buffer) {
  if (input.length === 0) throw badRequest("Empty file");
  if (input.length > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) throw new ServiceError(413, "too_large", "Photos must be 5 MB or smaller");
  if (!sniffImage(input)) throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "Only JPEG, PNG or WebP images are allowed");
  try {
    return await sharp(input, { failOn: "error", limitInputPixels: 50_000_000 })
      .rotate()
      .resize({ width: AVATAR_SIZE, height: AVATAR_SIZE, fit: "cover" })
      .jpeg({ quality: 85 })
      .toBuffer();
  } catch {
    throw new ServiceError(415, "unsupported_type", "That image couldn't be read. Try a JPEG, PNG or WebP photo.");
  }
}

export async function setAvatar(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, bytes: Buffer, now = new Date(), storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const clean = await processAvatar(bytes);
  const before = await db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId }, select: { avatarKey: true } });
  const key = newStorageKey("avatars", "jpg");
  await storage.put(key, clean, "image/jpeg");
  try {
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { avatarKey: key } });
  } catch (err) {
    await storage.delete(key).catch(() => {});
    throw err;
  }
  if (before.avatarKey) await storage.delete(before.avatarKey).catch(() => {});
  return { avatarUrl: await avatarUrl(key, now) };
}

export async function removeAvatar(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, storage: BlobStorage = getStorage()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const before = await db.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: me.userId }, select: { avatarKey: true } });
  if (before.avatarKey) {
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: me.userId }, data: { avatarKey: null } });
    await storage.delete(before.avatarKey).catch(() => {});
  }
  return { avatarUrl: null };
}

export type PersonWithState = PersonView & { follow: FollowState; isSelf: boolean };


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/posts.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Posts: a photo with a 1-10 rating, taken at the venue (see photos.ts#submitPhoto). The permanent, profile-level
 * version of being somewhere. Live reports are separate and unchanged, and posts never feed consensus, counts,
 * accuracy or reputation.
 *
 *  - Rating and caption can be added or changed until the end of the photo's night (5am America/New_York). Then the
 *    rating locks; the caption stays editable unless a moderator is looking at it (an open flag) or it's hidden.
 *    Adding a rating to a live-only photo that night turns it into a post.
 *  - "Share to venue page": shared posts appear in the venue feed and can be liked and featured. Unshared posts
 *    appear only on the author's profile.
 *  - "Delete from profile" hides the post everywhere (`removed_at`, final). Nothing is deleted (invariant 5).
 *  - Who can see a post is decided by src/server/privacy.ts.
 */
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { nightBounds } from "@/lib/time";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { assertCleanText, badRequest, conflict, forbidden, notFound, ServiceError } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertCanEditOwnContent, isModerator, ownsVenue, requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { authorResolver, canSeePhoto, subjectSelect, toSubject } from "@/server/privacy";
import { POST_CAPTION_MAX } from "@/server/services/photos";
import { profilePostsWhere } from "@/server/services/people";
import { isBlockedEitherWay } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/services/venues";
import { regularsAt } from "@/server/services/regulars";
import { likeSummaries } from "@/server/services/likes";

/** When the rating (and the chance to add one) locks: the end of the photo's night. */
export const ratingLocksAt = (night: string) => nightBounds(night).end;

export const postEditSchema = z
  .object({
    rating: z.number().int().min(1, "Rating: 1 to 10").max(10, "Rating: 1 to 10"),
    caption: z.string().trim().max(POST_CAPTION_MAX, `Caption: ${POST_CAPTION_MAX} characters max`),
    shared: z.boolean(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict()
  .refine((v) => Object.keys(v).length > 0, "Nothing to change");

/** Author edits: rating (until the night ends), caption (unless under review), shared. */
export async function editPost(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, photoId: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  requireSignedIn(actor);
  const input = postEditSchema.parse(raw);
  assertCleanText({ caption: input.caption });
  const photo = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: photoId } });
  if (!photo || photo.removedAt) throw notFound("Post not found");
  assertCanEditOwnContent(actor, photo);
  if (photo.hiddenAt) throw conflict("This post was hidden by a moderator and can't be edited");

  const ratingChanges = input.rating !== undefined && input.rating !== photo.rating;
  if (ratingChanges && now >= ratingLocksAt(photo.night)) {
    throw conflict(photo.rating === null ? "You can only rate a photo on the night you took it" : "Ratings lock when the night ends (5am)");
  }
  // Owners can't post for their own venue, and rating a live photo would make it a post there.
  if (ratingChanges && actor && ownsVenue(actor, photo.venueId)) throw forbidden("Venue accounts can't post for their own venue");
  const rating = input.rating ?? photo.rating;
  if (input.shared !== undefined && rating === null) throw badRequest("Rate it first: a photo without a rating is a live photo and always appears on the venue page");
  const captionChanges = input.caption !== undefined && input.caption !== photo.caption;
  // Live photos work as before: their caption is fixed once posted, unless this edit rates them (making a post).
  if (captionChanges && rating === null) throw badRequest("A live photo's caption can't be changed. Rate it to make it a post first.");
  if (captionChanges && photo.rating === null && input.caption!.length > POST_CAPTION_MAX) throw badRequest(`Caption: ${POST_CAPTION_MAX} characters max`);
  const sharedChanges = input.shared !== undefined && input.shared !== photo.shared;
  if (captionChanges || sharedChanges) {
    // Freeze while a moderator is looking at it, so the post can't be rewritten or moved out of view mid-review.
    const openFlags = await db.flag.count({ where: { targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: photo.id, status: "OPEN" } });
    if (openFlags > 0) throw conflict("This post is under moderator review, so it can't be edited right now");
  }
  // Turning a live photo into a post keeps it on the venue page (it's already there); the author can unshare after.
  return db.photo.update({
    where: { id: photo.id },
    data: {
      ...(ratingChanges ? { rating: input.rating } : {}),
      ...(captionChanges ? { caption: input.caption, editedAt: now } : {}),
      ...(sharedChanges ? { shared: input.shared } : {}),
    },
  });
}

/** "Delete from profile": hides the post everywhere for good. Only posts; live-only photos expire on their own. */
export async function removePost(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, photoId: string, now = new Date()) {
  requireSignedIn(actor);
  const photo = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: photoId } });
  if (!photo || photo.removedAt) throw notFound("Post not found");
  assertCanEditOwnContent(actor, photo);
  if (photo.rating === null) throw badRequest("Only posts can be removed");
  await db.photo.update({ where: { id: photo.id }, data: { removedAt: now } });
  return { removed: true as const };
}

const postSelect = {
  id: true, userId: true, venueId: true, storageKey: true, caption: true, rating: true, shared: true, night: true, createdAt: true, editedAt: true,
  hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true,
  user: { select: subjectSelect },
  venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } },
} as const;

/** Post detail. 404 when the viewer may not see it (privacy.ts#canSeePhoto), so existence isn't revealed. */
export async function getPost(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, photoId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const p = await db.photo.findUnique({ where: { id: photoId }, select: postSelect });
  if (!p || p.rating === null) throw notFound("Post not found");
  const resolve = await authorResolver(db, actor, [p.userId]);
  const subject = { authorId: p.userId, rating: p.rating, shared: p.shared, hidden: p.hiddenAt !== null, removed: p.removedAt !== null, countable: p.countable };
  if ((p.user.deletedAt || p.user.suspendedAt) && !resolve.viewer?.staff) throw notFound("Post not found");
  if (!canSeePhoto(resolve.viewer, subject, resolve.access(toSubject(p.user)), resolve.blocks.has(p.userId))) throw notFound("Post not found");
  const mine = actor?.userId === p.userId;
  return {
    post: {
      id: p.id,
      url: await uploadUrl(p.storageKey, now),
      rating: p.rating,
      caption: p.caption,
      shared: p.shared,
      createdAt: p.createdAt,
      editedAt: p.editedAt,
      author: resolve.author(toSubject(p.user), (await regularsAt(db, p.venueId, [p.userId], now)).has(p.userId)),
      venue: p.venue,
      mine,
      /** Your own post: until when the rating can change (the end of its night). null for everyone else. */
      ratingEditableUntil: mine ? ratingLocksAt(p.night) : null,
      /** Unshared posts can't be liked (null). */
      likes: p.shared ? (await likeSummaries(db, [p.id], actor?.userId)).get(p.id)! : null,
    },
  };
}

/** Access to a person's posts and places: the full profile (403 `private_account` otherwise), and no block. */
async function assertProfileVisible(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string) {
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: subjectSelect });
  if (!user || user.deletedAt || (user.suspendedAt && !isModerator(actor))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const isSelf = actor?.userId === user.id;
  if (actor && !isSelf && (await isBlockedEitherWay(db, actor.userId, user.id))) throw notFound("User not found");
  const resolve = await authorResolver(db, actor, [user.id]);
  if (resolve.access(toSubject(user)) === "limited") {
    throw new ServiceError(403, "private_account", "This account is private. Follow it to see their posts.");
  }
  return { user, isSelf };
}

export const POSTS_PAGE = 30;

/** Page cursor: the last post's time and id, so posts sharing a millisecond are never skipped. */
const cursorOf = (p: { createdAt: Date; id: string }) => `${p.createdAt.toISOString()}_${p.id}`;
function parseCursor(before: string) {
  const m = /^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T[\d:.]+Z)_([A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64})$/.exec(before);
  const at = m ? new Date(m[1]!) : null;
  if (!m || !at || Number.isNaN(at.getTime())) throw badRequest("before: use the previous page's nextBefore");
  return { at, id: m[2]! };
}

/** A profile's posts grid, newest first, 30 at a time (`before` = the previous page's `nextBefore`). */
export async function listUserPosts(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, before?: string | null, now = new Date()) {
  const { user, isSelf } = await assertProfileVisible(db, actor, handle);
  const c = before ? parseCursor(before) : null;
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: {
      ...profilePostsWhere(user.id, isSelf),
      ...(c ? { OR: [{ createdAt: { lt: c.at } }, { createdAt: c.at, id: { lt: c.id } }] } : {}),
    },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    take: POSTS_PAGE + 1,
    select: { id: true, storageKey: true, rating: true, shared: true, createdAt: true, venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } },
  });
  const page = rows.slice(0, POSTS_PAGE);
  return {
    posts: await Promise.all(
      page.map(async (p) => ({ id: p.id, url: await uploadUrl(p.storageKey, now), rating: p.rating!, shared: p.shared, createdAt: p.createdAt, venue: p.venue })),
    ),
    nextBefore: rows.length > POSTS_PAGE ? cursorOf(page[page.length - 1]!) : null,
  };
}

/** Every venue a person has posted at (the Places tab), with their latest rating there. Newest first. */
export async function listUserPlaces(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string) {
  const { user, isSelf } = await assertProfileVisible(db, actor, handle);
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: profilePostsWhere(user.id, isSelf),
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: { rating: true, createdAt: true, venueId: true, venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true, lat: true, lng: true, neighborhood: true } } },
  });
  const places = new Map<string, { venue: (typeof rows)[number]["venue"]; posts: number; lastRating: number; lastPostedAt: Date }>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    const p = places.get(r.venueId);
    if (p) p.posts++;
    else places.set(r.venueId, { venue: r.venue, posts: 1, lastRating: r.rating!, lastPostedAt: r.createdAt });
  }
  return { places: [...places.values()] };
}



====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/likes.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Likes on shared posts. Anyone signed in can like a shared post they can see (on the venue page or the author's
 * profile), once; never their own; unlike is allowed. Counts exclude App Review likes. Likes never touch
 * consensus, counts, accuracy or reputation; only eligible ones (src/lib/likes.ts) count toward featuring, and
 * only in the nightly job.
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { authorResolver, canSeePhoto, subjectSelect, toSubject } from "@/server/privacy";
import { consumeRate } from "@/server/rate-limit";

export type LikeSummary = { count: number; viewerLiked: boolean };

/** Like counts (App Review likes excluded) and whether the viewer liked each photo. */
export async function likeSummaries(db: Db, photoIds: readonly string[], viewerId: string | null | undefined): Promise<Map<string, LikeSummary>> {
  const ids = [...new Set(photoIds)];
  const out = new Map<string, LikeSummary>(ids.map((id) => [id, { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }]));
  if (!ids.length) return out;
  const [counts, mine] = await Promise.all([
    // Suspended accounts' likes don't show in counts (deleted accounts' likes are gone).
    db.photoLike.groupBy({ by: ["photoId"], where: { photoId: { in: ids }, countable: true, user: { suspendedAt: null } }, _count: { _all: true } }),
    viewerId ? db.photoLike.findMany({ where: { photoId: { in: ids }, userId: viewerId }, select: { photoId: true } }) : Promise.resolve([]),
  ]);
  for (const c of counts) out.get(c.photoId)!.count = c._count._all;
  for (const m of mine) out.get(m.photoId)!.viewerLiked = true;
  return out;
}

/** A shared post the viewer can see, or 404 (the same rule as post detail). */
async function likeablePost(db: Db, actor: Actor, photoId: string) {
  const p = await db.photo.findUnique({
    where: { id: photoId },
    select: { id: true, userId: true, rating: true, shared: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true, user: { select: subjectSelect } },
  });
  if (!p || p.rating === null || !p.shared || p.user.deletedAt || p.user.suspendedAt) throw notFound("Post not found");
  const resolve = await authorResolver(db, actor, [p.userId]);
  const subject = { authorId: p.userId, rating: p.rating, shared: p.shared, hidden: p.hiddenAt !== null, removed: p.removedAt !== null, countable: p.countable };
  // Staff pass canSeePhoto for hidden posts; nobody likes a hidden or removed post.
  if (subject.hidden || subject.removed || !canSeePhoto(resolve.viewer, subject, resolve.access(toSubject(p.user)), resolve.blocks.has(p.userId))) {
    throw notFound("Post not found");
  }
  return p;
}

export async function likePost(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, photoId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const post = await likeablePost(db, me, photoId);
  if (post.userId === me.userId) throw badRequest("You can't like your own post");
  await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Per (user, post) lock: a double tap can't hit the unique key and 500.
    await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`like:${me.userId}:${photoId}`}))`;
    const existing = await tx.photoLike.findUnique({ where: { userId_photoId: { userId: me.userId, photoId } } });
    if (existing) return;
    await consumeRate(tx, me.userId, "like", now);
    await tx.photoLike.create({ data: { userId: me.userId, photoId, createdAt: now, countable: !me.geofenceExempt } });
  });
  return { liked: true, ...(await likeSummaries(db, [photoId], me.userId)).get(photoId)! };
}

export async function unlikePost(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, photoId: string) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  // Unliking works even if the post has since become invisible to you (e.g. unshared): it only removes your own row.
  await db.photoLike.deleteMany({ where: { userId: me.userId, photoId } });
  // The count is only reported for a post you can still see.
  const visible = await likeablePost(db, me, photoId).then(() => true, () => false);
  return { liked: false, ...(visible ? (await likeSummaries(db, [photoId], me.userId)).get(photoId)! : { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }) };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/featuring.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Nightly featuring. Runs just after each night ends (5:05am America/New_York, POST /api/cron/featured).
 *
 * For every completed night in the last 7 that hasn't been processed, and every venue: rank that night's shared,
 * visible posts by eligible likes (src/lib/likes.ts) and store the top 20 with at least 3 eligible likes, with their
 * rank. The night is marked in `featured_run` in the same transaction.
 *
 * Deterministic whenever it runs: likes and account age are judged at the night's cutoff (5:05am, the scheduled
 * time), so a late or catch-up run features exactly what an on-time run would have (reputation, ownership and
 * suspension are read at run time). Idempotent and safe twice: each night is processed in one transaction that
 * first takes that night's advisory lock and skips a night already in `featured_run`; both tables are append-only
 * with database guards. One night failing (e.g. a moderator hides a photo mid-run) doesn't stop the others; the next
 * run retries it. Featuring never touches the live feed's order, consensus, counts or reputation, and venues can't
 * influence it.
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { FEATURE_CUTOFF_AFTER_NIGHT_MS, isEligibleLike, rankNight, type Liker } from "@/lib/likes";
import { addNights, nightBounds, nightKey } from "@/lib/time";

/** How far back a late or missed run catches up. */
export const FEATURING_CATCHUP_NIGHTS = 7;

/** Likes count until then: the night's end plus 5 minutes. */
export const featuringCutoff = (night: string) => new Date(nightBounds(night).end.getTime() + FEATURE_CUTOFF_AFTER_NIGHT_MS);

/**
 * Completed nights (ended by `now`) within the catch-up window, oldest first. Walks night keys by calendar day, so
 * daylight saving time can't skip a night (subtracting 24 hours across spring-forward would).
 */
export function completedNights(now: Date): string[] {
  const today = nightKey(now);
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (let d = FEATURING_CATCHUP_NIGHTS; d >= 1; d--) {
    const key = addNights(today, -d);
    if (nightBounds(key).end <= now) out.push(key);
  }
  return out;
}

type NightResult = { status: "done"; featured: number } | { status: "already" } | { status: "busy" };

async function featureNight(db: Db, night: string, now: Date): Promise<NightResult> {
  const cutoff = featuringCutoff(night);
  return db.$transaction(
    async (tx) => {
      // The lock and the work share this transaction's connection (safe behind PgBouncer in transaction mode).
      const got = await tx.$queryRaw<{ locked: boolean }[]>`SELECT pg_try_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`featuring:${night}`})) AS locked`;
      if (!got[0]?.locked) return { status: "busy" as const };
      if (await tx.featuredRun.findUnique({ where: { night } })) return { status: "already" as const };
      const posts = await tx.photo.findMany({
        where: { night, rating: { not: null }, shared: true, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null, countable: true, user: { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null } },
        select: {
          id: true, userId: true, venueId: true, createdAt: true,
          likes: {
            where: { createdAt: { lte: cutoff } },
            select: {
              countable: true, createdAt: true,
              user: {
                select: {
                  id: true, role: true, createdAt: true, reputation: true, deletedAt: true, suspendedAt: true, geofenceExempt: true,
                  claims: { where: { status: "APPROVED" }, select: { venueId: true } },
                },
              },
            },
          },
        },
      });
      const byVenue = new Map<string, { id: string; createdAt: Date; eligibleLikes: number }[]>();
      for (const p of posts) {
        const eligibleLikes = p.likes.filter((l) => {
          const liker: Liker = {
            id: l.user.id, role: l.user.role, createdAt: l.user.createdAt, reputation: l.user.reputation, geofenceExempt: l.user.geofenceExempt,
            deleted: l.user.deletedAt !== null, suspended: l.user.suspendedAt !== null, ownedVenueIds: l.user.claims.map((c) => c.venueId),
          };
          return isEligibleLike(l, liker, { authorId: p.userId, venueId: p.venueId }, cutoff);
        }).length;
        byVenue.set(p.venueId, [...(byVenue.get(p.venueId) ?? []), { id: p.id, createdAt: p.createdAt, eligibleLikes }]);
      }
      const rows = [...byVenue].flatMap(([venueId, candidates]) =>
        rankNight(candidates).map((r) => ({ venueId, night, photoId: r.id, rank: r.rank, eligibleLikes: r.eligibleLikes, createdAt: now })),
      );
      await tx.featuredRun.create({ data: { night, ranAt: now, featured: rows.length } });
      if (rows.length) await tx.featuredPhoto.createMany({ data: rows });
      return { status: "done" as const, featured: rows.length };
    },
    { timeout: 60_000 },
  );
}

/**
 * Processes every completed, unprocessed night in the catch-up window. `nights` = nights this run featured;
 * `skipped` = another run was processing a night at the same time; `failed` = nights that errored (retried next run).
 */
export async function runFeaturing(db: Db, now = new Date()) {
  const done = new Set((await db.featuredRun.findMany({ where: { night: { in: completedNights(now) } }, select: { night: true } })).map((r) => r.night));
  const nights: string[] = [];
  const failed: string[] = [];
  let featured = 0;
  let skipped = false;
  for (const night of completedNights(now).filter((n) => !done.has(n))) {
    try {
      const r = await featureNight(db, night, now);
      if (r.status === "busy") skipped = true;
      if (r.status === "done") {
        nights.push(night);
        featured += r.featured;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`[featuring] night ${night} failed; the next run retries it`, err);
      failed.push(night);
    }
  }
  return { nights, featured, skipped, failed };
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/gallery.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * The featured gallery on a bar's page: "This month's best" (every featured photo from nights in the current
 * calendar month, New York time, newest night first) and an archive of past months ("September's best").
 * Each night shows its top 10 still-visible featured photos: if a moderator hides one (or its author removes or
 * unshares it), the next-ranked one takes its place. Venues can't pick, hide or reorder any of it; every photo keeps
 * Report. Authors are resolved per viewer, and a viewer never sees photos by someone they blocked (or who blocked them).
 */
import { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { visibleTop } from "@/lib/likes";
import { localParts } from "@/lib/time";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { badRequest, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { authorResolver, subjectSelect, toSubject, type SubjectRow } from "@/server/privacy";
import { likeSummaries } from "@/server/services/likes";
import { regularsAt } from "@/server/services/regulars";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/services/venues";

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

/** "YYYY-MM" of the current New York calendar month. */
export const currentMonth = (now: Date) => {
  const p = localParts(now);
  return `${p.year}-${pad(p.month)}`;
};

/** Photos on this month's preview on the venue page; the rest is on the month screen. */
export const MONTH_PREVIEW = 30;

const featuredSelect = {
  night: true, rank: true,
  photo: {
    select: {
      id: true, userId: true, storageKey: true, caption: true, createdAt: true, rating: true, shared: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true,
      user: { select: subjectSelect },
    },
  },
} as const;

type FeaturedRow = {
  night: string;
  rank: number;
  photo: {
    id: string; userId: string; storageKey: string; caption: string; createdAt: Date; rating: number | null; shared: boolean;
    hiddenAt: Date | null; removedAt: Date | null; countable: boolean; user: SubjectRow;
  };
};

/** Still visible to everyone: shared, not hidden or removed, not App Review, author not deleted or suspended. */
const stillVisible = (r: FeaturedRow) =>
  r.photo.shared && !r.photo.hiddenAt && !r.photo.removedAt && r.photo.countable && !r.photo.user.deletedAt && !r.photo.user.suspendedAt;

/** Group by night (newest first) and keep each night's top 10 visible to this viewer. */
function topPerNight(rows: FeaturedRow[], blocked: Set<string>) {
  const nights = new Map<string, FeaturedRow[]>();
  for (const r of rows) nights.set(r.night, [...(nights.get(r.night) ?? []), r]);
  return [...nights]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? 1 : -1))
    .map(([night, list]) => ({ night, rows: visibleTop(list, (r) => stillVisible(r) && !blocked.has(r.photo.userId)) }));
}

type Resolver = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof authorResolver>>;

async function views(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, resolve: Resolver, venueId: string, nights: { night: string; rows: FeaturedRow[] }[], now: Date) {
  const all = nights.flatMap((n) => n.rows);
  const authorIds = all.map((r) => r.photo.userId);
  const [regulars, likes] = await Promise.all([regularsAt(db, venueId, authorIds, now), likeSummaries(db, all.map((r) => r.photo.id), actor?.userId)]);
  return Promise.all(
    nights.map(async (n) => ({
      night: n.night,
      photos: await Promise.all(
        n.rows.map(async (r, i) => ({
          id: r.photo.id,
          url: await uploadUrl(r.photo.storageKey, now),
          caption: r.photo.caption,
          createdAt: r.photo.createdAt,
          rating: r.photo.rating,
          author: resolve.author(toSubject(r.photo.user), regulars.has(r.photo.userId)),
          mine: actor?.userId === r.photo.userId,
          likes: likes.get(r.photo.id)!,
          night: r.night,
          /** Position in this night's gallery (1-10), after any hidden photos were skipped. */
          rank: i + 1,
        })),
      ),
    })),
  );
}

/**
 * One month's gallery, newest night first. `limit` caps the photos built (signed URLs, likes, Regular status), for
 * the venue page's preview; `total` is the full count for the viewer.
 */
export async function featuredMonth(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, venueId: string, month: string, now = new Date(), limit = Infinity) {
  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month)) throw badRequest("month: YYYY-MM");
  const rows = (await db.featuredPhoto.findMany({ where: { venueId, night: { startsWith: `${month}-` } }, select: featuredSelect })) as FeaturedRow[];
  const resolve = await authorResolver(db, actor, rows.map((r) => r.photo.userId));
  const nights = topPerNight(rows, resolve.blocks);
  const total = nights.reduce((n, x) => n + x.rows.length, 0);
  // Keep the first `limit` photos (newest nights first), dropping emptied nights.
  let left = limit;
  const kept = nights.map((n) => {
    const take = n.rows.slice(0, Math.max(0, left));
    left -= take.length;
    return { night: n.night, rows: take };
  }).filter((n) => n.rows.length > 0);
  return { month, nights: await views(db, actor, resolve, venueId, kept, now), total };
}

/** Past months with featured photos, newest first, and how many each shows (before the viewer's blocks). */
export async function featuredArchive(db: Db, venueId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const before = `${currentMonth(now)}-01`;
  const rows = await db.$queryRaw<{ month: string; count: bigint }[]>(Prisma.sql`
    SELECT month, count(*) AS count FROM (
      SELECT substr(f.night, 1, 7) AS month, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY f.night ORDER BY f.rank) AS n
      FROM featured_photo f
      JOIN photo p ON p.id = f.photo_id
      JOIN app_user u ON u.id = p.user_id
      WHERE f.venue_id = ${venueId} AND f.night < ${before}
        AND p.shared AND p.hidden_at IS NULL AND p.removed_at IS NULL AND p.countable
        AND u.deleted_at IS NULL AND u.suspended_at IS NULL
    ) visible
    WHERE n <= 10
    GROUP BY month
    ORDER BY month DESC`);
  return rows.map((r) => ({ month: r.month, count: Number(r.count) }));
}

/** The venue page's gallery section: this month's preview plus the archive list. */
export async function venueGallery(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, venueId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const month = currentMonth(now);
  const [thisMonth, archive] = await Promise.all([featuredMonth(db, actor, venueId, month, now, MONTH_PREVIEW), featuredArchive(db, venueId, now)]);
  return { thisMonth: { month, photos: thisMonth.nights.flatMap((n) => n.photos), total: thisMonth.total }, archive };
}

/**
 * "Featured at Lantern & Lark, October" for a profile: the person's photos currently shown in a gallery (top 10
 * visible for their night), grouped by venue and month, newest first. Needs full profile access (the caller checks).
 */
export async function featuredHistory(db: Db, userId: string) {
  const mine = await db.featuredPhoto.findMany({ where: { photo: { userId } }, select: { venueId: true, night: true } });
  if (!mine.length) return [];
  const keys = [...new Set(mine.map((m) => `${m.venueId}|${m.night}`))];
  const rows = (await db.featuredPhoto.findMany({
    where: { OR: keys.map((k) => ({ venueId: k.split("|")[0]!, night: k.split("|")[1]! })) },
    select: { ...featuredSelect, venueId: true, venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } },
  })) as (FeaturedRow & { venueId: string; venue: { name: string; slug: string } })[];
  const out = new Map<string, { venue: { name: string; slug: string }; month: string; count: number }>();
  for (const key of keys) {
    const [venueId, night] = key.split("|") as [string, string];
    const shown = visibleTop(rows.filter((r) => r.venueId === venueId && r.night === night), stillVisible);
    const count = shown.filter((r) => r.photo.userId === userId).length;
    if (!count) continue;
    const month = night.slice(0, 7);
    const k = `${venueId}|${month}`;
    const entry = out.get(k) ?? { venue: shown[0]!.venue, month, count: 0 };
    entry.count += count;
    out.set(k, entry);
  }
  return [...out.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.month < b.month ? 1 : a.month > b.month ? -1 : a.venue.name.localeCompare(b.venue.name)));
}

export async function assertVenue(db: Db, idOrSlug: string) {
  const v = await db.venue.findFirst({ where: { OR: [{ id: idOrSlug }, { slug: idOrSlug }] }, select: { id: true } });
  if (!v) throw notFound("Venue not found");
  return v;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/activity.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Activity (in-app only, no push): follow requests, new followers, likes on your posts, and "You were featured at
 * ___". Derived from the data each time; there's no notifications table. People are shown as the rules allow
 * (src/server/privacy.ts), blocked people never appear, and App Review likes don't count.
 */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/time";
import { visibleTop } from "@/lib/likes";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { requireSignedIn } from "@/server/permissions";
import { personView, profileAccess, subjectSelect, toSubject, viewerOf } from "@/server/privacy";
import { blockedIdsFor } from "@/server/services/blocks";
import { avatarUrl } from "@/server/services/people";
import { followedAmong } from "@/server/privacy";
import { uploadUrl } from "@/server/services/venues";

export const ACTIVITY_DAYS = 30;
export const ACTIVITY_MAX = 100;

export async function listActivity(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()) {
  const me = requireSignedIn(actor);
  const since = new Date(now.getTime() - ACTIVITY_DAYS * DAY_MS);
  const blocked = await blockedIdsFor(db, me.userId);
  const active = { deletedAt: null, suspendedAt: null, id: { notIn: blocked } };
  const post = { select: { id: true, storageKey: true, venue: { select: { name: true, slug: true } } } } as const;
  const [requests, followers, likes, featured] = await Promise.all([
    db.followRequest.findMany({ where: { targetId: me.userId, requester: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: ACTIVITY_MAX, select: { createdAt: true, requester: { select: subjectSelect } } }),
    db.follow.findMany({ where: { followeeId: me.userId, createdAt: { gt: since }, follower: active }, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: ACTIVITY_MAX, select: { createdAt: true, follower: { select: subjectSelect } } }),
    db.photoLike.findMany({
      where: { photo: { userId: me.userId, removedAt: null, hiddenAt: null }, countable: true, createdAt: { gt: since }, user: active },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      take: ACTIVITY_MAX,
      select: { createdAt: true, user: { select: subjectSelect }, photo: post },
    }),
    db.featuredPhoto.findMany({
      where: { photo: { userId: me.userId }, createdAt: { gt: since } },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      take: ACTIVITY_MAX,
      select: { createdAt: true, night: true, venueId: true, photo: post },
    }),
  ]);

  // Only features still shown in the gallery (top 10 visible for that night): one query for all those nights.
  const nightsOf = featured.length
    ? await db.featuredPhoto.findMany({
        where: { OR: featured.map((f) => ({ venueId: f.venueId, night: f.night })) },
        select: { venueId: true, night: true, rank: true, photoId: true, photo: { select: { shared: true, hiddenAt: true, removedAt: true, countable: true, user: { select: { deletedAt: true, suspendedAt: true } } } } },
      })
    : [];
  const shownFeatures = featured.filter((f) =>
    visibleTop(
      nightsOf.filter((r) => r.venueId === f.venueId && r.night === f.night),
      (r) => r.photo.shared && !r.photo.hiddenAt && !r.photo.removedAt && r.photo.countable && !r.photo.user.deletedAt && !r.photo.user.suspendedAt,
    ).some((r) => r.photoId === f.photo.id),
  );

  const people = [...requests.map((r) => r.requester), ...followers.map((f) => f.follower), ...likes.map((l) => l.user)];
  const follows = await followedAmong(db, me.userId, people.map((p) => p.id));
  const viewer = viewerOf(me);
  const person = async (p: (typeof people)[number]) => {
    const access = profileAccess(viewer, toSubject(p), follows.has(p.id));
    return personView(access, { ...p, verified: p.verifiedAt !== null }, access === "full" ? await avatarUrl(p.avatarKey, now) : null);
  };
  const postRef = async (p: { id: string; storageKey: string; venue: { name: string; slug: string } }) => ({ id: p.id, url: await uploadUrl(p.storageKey, now), venue: p.venue });

  const items = [
    ...(await Promise.all(requests.map(async (r) => ({ kind: "follow_request" as const, at: r.createdAt, person: await person(r.requester) })))),
    ...(await Promise.all(followers.map(async (f) => ({ kind: "new_follower" as const, at: f.createdAt, person: await person(f.follower) })))),
    ...(await Promise.all(likes.map(async (l) => ({ kind: "like" as const, at: l.createdAt, person: await person(l.user), post: await postRef(l.photo) })))),
    ...(await Promise.all(shownFeatures.map(async (f) => ({ kind: "featured" as const, at: f.createdAt, night: f.night, post: await postRef(f.photo) })))),
  ];
  return items.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime()).slice(0, ACTIVITY_MAX);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/verification.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * The verified badge: handpicked by an admin, no application flow. Every grant and revoke needs a note and is
 * written to the append-only `verification_event` log (a trigger rejects UPDATE and DELETE) in the same transaction
 * as the change.
 *
 * Badge only: verification never changes ranking, featuring eligibility, consensus or anything else. Nothing reads
 * `verified_at` except the code that displays the badge.
 */
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Actor } from "@/server/actor";
import { conflict, notFound } from "@/server/errors";
import { assertAdmin } from "@/server/permissions";

/** Serializes badge changes for one account (the admin tools and account deletion). */
export async function lockVerification(tx: Prisma.TransactionClient, userId: string) {
  await tx.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${`verify:${userId}`}))`;
}

export const verificationSchema = z
  .object({
    verified: z.boolean(),
    note: z.string().trim().min(3, "Add a note (why)").max(300, "Note: 300 characters max"),
  })
  .strict();

export async function setVerified(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle: string, raw: unknown, now = new Date()) {
  const admin = assertAdmin(actor);
  const { verified, note } = verificationSchema.parse(raw);
  const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true } });
  if (!user) throw notFound("User not found");
  return db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    // Same lock as account deletion, and re-read under it: a grant can't land on an account being deleted.
    await lockVerification(tx, user.id);
    const current = await tx.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: user.id }, select: { verifiedAt: true, deletedAt: true } });
    if (current.deletedAt) throw notFound("User not found");
    if ((current.verifiedAt !== null) === verified) throw conflict(verified ? "Already verified" : "Not verified");
    // The log entry first: the database refuses a badge change without one in the same transaction.
    await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { userId: user.id, action: verified ? "GRANT" : "REVOKE", note, actorId: admin.userId, createdAt: now } });
    const updated = await tx.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { verifiedAt: verified ? now : null }, select: { handle: true, verifiedAt: true } });
    return { handle: updated.handle, verified: updated.verifiedAt !== null, verifiedAt: updated.verifiedAt };
  });
}

/** Admin user search for the verification tools. Staff see real accounts (email included), private or not. */
export async function adminSearchUsers(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, q: string) {
  assertAdmin(actor);
  const query = q.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^@/, "");
  if (query.length < 2) return [];
  const users = await db.user.findMany({
    where: {
      deletedAt: null,
      OR: [{ handle: { contains: query } }, { displayName: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" } }, { email: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" } }],
    },
    orderBy: { handle: "asc" },
    take: 25,
    select: { handle: true, displayName: true, email: true, role: true, isPrivate: true, verifiedAt: true, suspendedAt: true, createdAt: true },
  });
  return users.map((u) => ({
    handle: u.handle, displayName: u.displayName, email: u.email, role: u.role, isPrivate: u.isPrivate,
    verified: u.verifiedAt !== null, verifiedAt: u.verifiedAt, suspended: u.suspendedAt !== null, memberSince: u.createdAt,
  }));
}

/** The verification log, newest first (optionally for one account). */
export async function verificationLog(db: Db, actor: Actor | null, handle?: string | null, take = 100) {
  assertAdmin(actor);
  const user = handle ? await db.user.findUnique({ where: { handle: handle.toLowerCase() }, select: { id: true } }) : null;
  if (handle && !user) throw notFound("User not found");
  const rows = await db.verificationEvent.findMany({
    where: user ? { userId: user.id } : {},
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    take,
    include: { user: { select: { handle: true } }, actor: { select: { handle: true } } },
  });
  return rows.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, handle: e.user.handle, action: e.action, note: e.note, by: e.actor?.handle ?? "system", at: e.createdAt }));
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/regulars.ts
====================================================================================================

/** Regular status from the posts table (see src/lib/regulars.ts for the rule). */
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { regularPairs, regularWindowStart } from "@/lib/regulars";

/** Posts that count toward Regular status. */
const countingPosts = (now: Date) =>
  ({ rating: { not: null }, hiddenAt: null, removedAt: null, countable: true, night: { gte: regularWindowStart(now) } }) as const;

/** Which of `userIds` are Regulars at this venue. */
export async function regularsAt(db: Db, venueId: string, userIds: readonly string[], now = new Date()): Promise<Set<string>> {
  if (!userIds.length) return new Set();
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: { ...countingPosts(now), venueId, userId: { in: [...new Set(userIds)] } },
    select: { userId: true, venueId: true, night: true, createdAt: true },
  });
  const pairs = regularPairs(rows, now);
  return new Set(userIds.filter((id) => pairs.has(`${id}:${venueId}`)));
}

/** Venues where this person is a Regular, by name. */
export async function regularVenuesOf(db: Db, userId: string, now = new Date()) {
  const rows = await db.photo.findMany({
    where: { ...countingPosts(now), userId },
    select: { userId: true, venueId: true, night: true, createdAt: true },
  });
  const pairs = regularPairs(rows, now);
  const ids = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.venueId))].filter((v) => pairs.has(`${userId}:${v}`));
  if (!ids.length) return [];
  return db.venue.findMany({ where: { id: { in: ids } }, orderBy: { name: "asc" }, select: { name: true, slug: true } });
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/likes.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Likes and featuring rules, pure and in one place.
 *
 * Everyone signed in can like a shared post they can see. Only **eligible** likes count toward featuring:
 *  - from a USER account (not a venue account or staff) at least 7 days old with reputation at least 0.5;
 *  - not from the post's author, and not from an owner of that venue;
 *  - not from an App Review account (`countable = false` when liked, or flagged since), not deleted or suspended;
 *  - made by the night's cutoff: 5:05am, when the job is scheduled.
 * Account age is judged at the cutoff too, so a late or catch-up run gives the same result as an on-time one.
 * Reputation, ownership and suspension are read when the job runs (there's no history of them).
 *
 * Nightly featuring: for each venue and night, rank that night's shared, visible posts by eligible likes (ties:
 * the earlier post wins, then the smaller id), keep those with at least 3, and store the top 20 with their rank.
 * The gallery shows the top 10 still visible.
 */
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/time";

export const LIKE_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_MS = 7 * DAY_MS;
export const LIKE_MIN_REPUTATION = 0.5;
export const FEATURE_MIN_LIKES = 3;
export const FEATURE_STORE_TOP = 20;
export const FEATURE_SHOW_TOP = 10;
/** Likes count until this long after the night ends (the job's scheduled time). */
export const FEATURE_CUTOFF_AFTER_NIGHT_MS = 5 * 60_000;

export type Liker = {
  id: string;
  role: "USER" | "VENUE_OWNER" | "MODERATOR" | "ADMIN";
  createdAt: Date;
  reputation: number;
  deleted: boolean;
  suspended: boolean;
