  /** App Review / QA account now (its likes never count, whenever it was flagged). */
  geofenceExempt: boolean;
  /** Venues with an approved claim (at run time). */
  ownedVenueIds: readonly string[];
};

export type LikeRow = { countable: boolean; createdAt: Date };
export type PostRef = { authorId: string; venueId: string };

/** `cutoff`: the night's end plus 5 minutes (see featuring.ts), not the moment the job happens to run. */
export function isEligibleLike(like: LikeRow, liker: Liker, post: PostRef, cutoff: Date): boolean {
  if (!like.countable || like.createdAt > cutoff) return false;
  if (liker.deleted || liker.suspended || liker.geofenceExempt) return false;
  if (liker.role !== "USER") return false;
  if (liker.createdAt.getTime() > cutoff.getTime() - LIKE_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_MS) return false;
  if (liker.reputation < LIKE_MIN_REPUTATION) return false;
  if (liker.id === post.authorId) return false;
  if (liker.ownedVenueIds.includes(post.venueId)) return false;
  return true;
}

export type Candidate = { id: string; createdAt: Date; eligibleLikes: number };

/** One venue-night: posts with at least 3 eligible likes, best first, top 20, ranked 1..n. */
export function rankNight(posts: readonly Candidate[]): (Candidate & { rank: number })[] {
  return posts
    .filter((p) => p.eligibleLikes >= FEATURE_MIN_LIKES)
    .sort((a, b) => b.eligibleLikes - a.eligibleLikes || a.createdAt.getTime() - b.createdAt.getTime() || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0))
    .slice(0, FEATURE_STORE_TOP)
    .map((p, i) => ({ ...p, rank: i + 1 }));
}

/** The gallery for one night: stored ranks in order, skipping ones no longer visible, top 10. */
export function visibleTop<T extends { rank: number }>(rows: readonly T[], isVisible: (row: T) => boolean): T[] {
  return [...rows].sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank).filter(isVisible).slice(0, FEATURE_SHOW_TOP);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/regulars.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * "Regular at ___": a person is a Regular at a venue when they have posts there on at least 3 different nights
 * within the last 60 days (tonight and the 59 before it). Computed from posts every time (never stored or
 * editable), like the accuracy score. Only posts count (rated photos), and only visible ones: hidden, removed and
 * App Review posts don't.
 *
 * A "night" is a separate visit: a post only opens a new night if it's on a different night *and* at least 12 hours
 * after the last counted one. Otherwise one visit spanning 5am (a post at 4:50am and another at 5:06am, 16 minutes
 * apart) would count as two nights.
 */
import { addNights, HOUR_MS, nightKey } from "@/lib/time";

export { addNights };

export const REGULAR_MIN_NIGHTS = 3;
export const REGULAR_WINDOW_DAYS = 60;
/** Two counted nights must be at least this far apart (one visit can't straddle 5am into two nights). */
export const REGULAR_MIN_GAP_MS = 12 * HOUR_MS;

/** The earliest night in the window ending tonight: exactly 60 nights, tonight included. */
export function regularWindowStart(now: Date): string {
  return addNights(nightKey(now), -(REGULAR_WINDOW_DAYS - 1));
}

type PostRow = { userId: string; venueId: string; night: string; createdAt: Date };

/** Separate nights (visits) among one person's posts at one venue, inside the window. */
export function countVisits(rows: readonly PostRow[], now: Date): number {
  const from = regularWindowStart(now);
  const today = nightKey(now);
  const inWindow = rows.filter((r) => r.night >= from && r.night <= today).sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.getTime() - b.createdAt.getTime());
  const nights = new Set<string>();
  let last: Date | null = null;
  for (const r of inWindow) {
    if (nights.has(r.night)) continue;
    if (last && r.createdAt.getTime() - last.getTime() < REGULAR_MIN_GAP_MS) continue;
    nights.add(r.night);
    last = r.createdAt;
  }
  return nights.size;
}

/** "userId:venueId" for every Regular pair among the rows. */
export function regularPairs(rows: readonly PostRow[], now: Date): Set<string> {
  const groups = new Map<string, PostRow[]>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    const key = `${r.userId}:${r.venueId}`;
    groups.set(key, [...(groups.get(key) ?? []), r]);
  }
  return new Set([...groups].filter(([, g]) => countVisits(g, now) >= REGULAR_MIN_NIGHTS).map(([k]) => k));
}


====================================================================================================
== prisma/seed-social.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * The social layer's demo data, called by seed.ts after reputations are computed (featuring reads them).
 *
 * Deterministic: its own fixed-seed PRNG (so the rest of the seed is unchanged), explicit ids, and every time derived
 * from the anchor. Featured galleries come from the real nightly job (`runFeaturing`), run once per past week, so they
 * follow exactly the production rules (eligible likes, top 20 stored, 3-like minimum).
 *
 * The mix: public and private accounts with bios and some profile photos; pending follow requests (four waiting on
 * the demo account, which is private); rated posts at many venues since the start of the month two months back; a few
 * Regulars (3+ nights at one venue in 60); likes; featured photos for the two past months and this one; verified
 * accounts (log entry then badge, in one transaction); a private author whose shared photo reads "Anonymous" to
 * people who don't follow them; one removed post; one featured photo hidden by a moderator (the next one moves up).
 */
import type { Prisma, PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
import type { BlobStorage } from "../src/lib/storage";
import { mulberry32 } from "../src/lib/rng";
import { runFeaturing } from "../src/server/services/featuring";
import { addNights, HOUR_MS, localParts, MINUTE_MS, nightBounds, nightKey, zonedTimeToUtc } from "../src/lib/time";
import { placeholderPng } from "./placeholder-image";
import type { VenueFixture } from "./seed-data";

type SeedUser = { id: string; handle: string; createdAt?: Date | string | null };

export type SocialContext = {
  db: PrismaClient;
  storage: BlobStorage;
  now: Date;
  venues: readonly VenueFixture[];
  venueId: (slug: string) => string;
  regulars: SeedUser[];
  demo: SeedUser;
  admin: SeedUser;
  mod: SeedUser;
};

const BIOS = [
  "Mostly on Avenue A. Here for the jukebox.",
  "Bartender by day, regular by night.",
  "Rating every negroni in the East Village.",
  "LES after midnight.",
  "If there's a patio, I'm on it.",
  "Dance floor first, questions later.",
];
const POST_CAPTIONS = [
  "Best negroni on the block",
  "Packed but worth it",
  "The DJ went off tonight",
  "Quiet corner, great drinks",
  "Back patio is open",
  "Line moved fast",
  "Live band until 1",
  "",
  "",
];

let n = 0;
// Its own id prefix, so these never collide with seed.ts's counter.
const id = (prefix: string) => `seed_s_${prefix}_${String(++n).padStart(6, "0")}`;

export async function seedSocial(ctx: SocialContext) {
  const { db, storage, now, venues, venueId, regulars, demo, admin, mod } = ctx;
  const r = mulberry32(20260927);
  const at = (u: SeedUser) => new Date(u.createdAt ?? 0).getTime();
  const tonight = nightKey(now);
  // Start of the month two months back ("July" when it's September), in New York.
  const p = localParts(now);
  const startMonth = p.month - 2 <= 0 ? p.month + 10 : p.month - 2;
  const startYear = p.month - 2 <= 0 ? p.year - 1 : p.year;
  const firstNight = `${startYear}-${String(startMonth).padStart(2, "0")}-01`;
  const tonightStart = nightBounds(tonight).start.getTime();
  /** Tonight's posts: a few minutes to hours ago, but never before tonight started (5am). */
  const earlierTonight = (minutes: number) => new Date(Math.min(now.getTime(), Math.max(now.getTime() - minutes * MINUTE_MS, tonightStart + MINUTE_MS)));

  // The demo account has posts, a Regular badge and a featured photo going back months: it must be older than they are.
  const demoCreated = new Date(now.getTime() - 150 * 24 * HOUR_MS);
  await db.user.update({ where: { id: demo.id }, data: { createdAt: demoCreated, termsAcceptedAt: demoCreated } });
  demo.createdAt = demoCreated;

  // ---------------- Profiles: public / private, bios, photos ----------------
  // About 40% public. The demo account stays private (it has requests waiting); regulars[4] is the private author
  // whose shared photo shows as Anonymous.
  const anon = regulars[4]!;
  const isPublic = (u: SeedUser, i: number) => u.id !== demo.id && u.id !== anon.id && (i % 5 === 1 || i % 5 === 3);
  for (const [i, u] of regulars.entries()) {
    const data: Prisma.UserUpdateInput = { isPrivate: !isPublic(u, i) };
    if (i % 3 === 0) data.bio = BIOS[i % BIOS.length]!;
    if (i % 4 === 1 || u.id === demo.id) {
      const key = `avatars/seed-${u.handle}.png`;
      await storage.put(key, placeholderPng(r, 240, 240), "image/png");
      data.avatarKey = key;
    }
    await db.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data });
  }
  const publicIds = new Set(regulars.filter((u, i) => isPublic(u, i)).map((u) => u.id));

  // ---------------- Verified (the log entry first; the database refuses a badge without one) ----------------
  const verified = [regulars[1]!, regulars[6]!, regulars[13]!];
  for (const u of verified) {
    const when = new Date(now.getTime() - r.int(10, 60) * 24 * HOUR_MS);
    await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.verificationEvent.create({ data: { id: id("ve"), userId: u.id, action: "GRANT", note: "Local nightlife writer (seed)", actorId: admin.id, createdAt: when } });
      await tx.user.update({ where: { id: u.id }, data: { verifiedAt: when } });
    });
  }

  // ---------------- Pending follow requests ----------------
  // One explicit follower of the private author, so "@handle to followers, Anonymous to others" doesn't depend on
  // seed.ts's random follows.
  const fan = regulars[2]!;
  await db.follow.createMany({ data: [{ followerId: fan.id, followeeId: anon.id, createdAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 20 * 24 * HOUR_MS) }], skipDuplicates: true });
  const follows = await db.follow.findMany({ select: { followerId: true, followeeId: true } });
  const following = new Set(follows.map((f) => `${f.followerId}>${f.followeeId}`));
  const requests: Prisma.FollowRequestCreateManyInput[] = [];
  const request = (from: SeedUser, to: SeedUser, minutesAgo: number) => {
    const key = `${from.id}>${to.id}`;
    if (from.id === to.id || following.has(key) || requests.some((q) => q.requesterId === from.id && q.targetId === to.id)) return false;
    // Never before either account existed.
    const when = Math.max(now.getTime() - minutesAgo * MINUTE_MS, at(from) + HOUR_MS, at(to) + HOUR_MS);
    if (when > now.getTime()) return false;
    requests.push({ requesterId: from.id, targetId: to.id, createdAt: new Date(when) });
    return true;
  };
  // Four on the demo account: going public offers "Approve 4 pending requests?".
  let onDemo = 0;
  for (const u of r.shuffle(regulars)) if (onDemo < 4 && request(u, demo, r.int(30, 60 * 72))) onDemo++;
  // A handful elsewhere, to private accounts only (following a public account is instant).
  const privates = regulars.filter((u) => !publicIds.has(u.id) && u.id !== demo.id);
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) request(r.pick(regulars), r.pick(privates), r.int(60, 60 * 24 * 10));
  await db.followRequest.createMany({ data: requests });

  // ---------------- Posts ----------------
  const posters = regulars.filter((u) => now.getTime() - at(u) > 14 * 24 * HOUR_MS);
  const photos: (Prisma.PhotoCreateManyInput & { id: string; userId: string; createdAt: Date; shared: boolean })[] = [];
  const nightTime = (night: string, minutesAfter10pm: number) => {
    const [y, m, d] = night.split("-").map(Number) as [number, number, number];
    return new Date(zonedTimeToUtc(y, m, d, 22).getTime() + minutesAfter10pm * MINUTE_MS);
  };
  const venue = (slug: string) => venues.find((v) => v.slug === slug)!;
  const near = (v: VenueFixture) => ({ lat: v.lat + (r.next() - 0.5) * 0.0006, lng: v.lng + (r.next() - 0.5) * 0.0007 });
  const post = async (u: SeedUser, slug: string, createdAt: Date, opts: { shared?: boolean; rating?: number; removed?: boolean } = {}) => {
    const v = venue(slug);
    const pid = id("sp");
    const key = `photos/seed-post-${pid}.png`;
    await storage.put(key, placeholderPng(r), "image/png");
    const shared = opts.shared ?? (publicIds.has(u.id) ? r.chance(0.9) : r.chance(0.25));
    const row = {
      id: pid, userId: u.id, venueId: venueId(slug), storageKey: key, mimeType: "image/png", caption: r.pick(POST_CAPTIONS), ...near(v),
      createdAt, rating: opts.rating ?? Number(r.weighted({ "6": 1, "7": 3, "8": 4, "9": 3, "10": 1 })), shared,
      removedAt: opts.removed ? new Date(createdAt.getTime() + HOUR_MS) : null,
    };
    photos.push(row);
    return row;
  };

  // Everyday posting: about one venue-night in eight has posts.
  for (let night = firstNight; night < tonight; night = addNights(night, 1)) {
    for (const v of venues) {
      if (!r.chance(0.12)) continue;
      const existing = posters.filter((u) => at(u) < nightTime(night, 0).getTime());
      for (const u of r.shuffle(existing).slice(0, r.int(1, 4))) await post(u, v.slug, nightTime(night, r.int(0, 200)));
    }
  }
  // Regulars: four long-standing accounts and the demo account, each with 3-4 separate nights at one venue in the last 50.
  const veterans = regulars.filter((u) => u.id !== demo.id && u.id !== anon.id && now.getTime() - at(u) > 90 * 24 * HOUR_MS);
  const regularPlan: [SeedUser, string][] = [
    [veterans[0]!, "lantern-and-lark"], [veterans[1]!, "velvet-anchor"], [veterans[2]!, "copper-fox"],
    [veterans[3]!, "delancey-echo"], [demo, "lantern-and-lark"],
  ];
  for (const [u, slug] of regularPlan) {
    const nights = r.shuffle(Array.from({ length: 45 }, (_, i) => addNights(tonight, -(i + 2)))).slice(0, r.int(3, 4));
    for (const night of nights) await post(u, slug, nightTime(night, r.int(0, 150)), { shared: publicIds.has(u.id) || u.id === demo.id ? true : undefined });
  }
  // The demo account's featured photo: a shared post early last month with plenty of likes.
  const lastMonthNight = `${p.month === 1 ? p.year - 1 : p.year}-${String(p.month === 1 ? 12 : p.month - 1).padStart(2, "0")}-08`;
  const demoStar = await post(demo, "copper-fox", nightTime(lastMonthNight, 60), { shared: true, rating: 9 });
  // And a recent one (within Activity's 30 days and the job's 7-night catch-up), so Activity shows likes and "featured".
  const demoRecent = await post(demo, "half-moon-hifi", nightTime(addNights(tonight, -3), 90), { shared: true, rating: 8 });
  const boosted = new Map([[demoStar.id, 12], [demoRecent.id, 7]]);
  // One post the demo account removed from its profile (hidden for good).
  await post(demo, "saint-marigold", nightTime(addNights(tonight, -9), 30), { shared: false, rating: 6, removed: true });
  // Tonight: rated posts in the live feed, including the private author's shared one (Anonymous to non-followers).
  const anonTonight = await post(anon, "velvet-anchor", earlierTonight(35), { shared: true, rating: 8 });
  for (const slug of ["velvet-anchor", "lantern-and-lark", "delancey-echo", "rivington-riot", "neon-oyster", "copper-fox"]) {
    for (const u of r.shuffle(posters).slice(0, r.int(1, 2))) await post(u, slug, earlierTonight(r.int(10, 160)));
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < photos.length; i += 500) await db.photo.createMany({ data: photos.slice(i, i + 500) });

  // ---------------- Likes ----------------
  // Likers existed before they liked; about half the posts get a few, a fifth get enough to be featured.
  const reps = new Map((await db.user.findMany({ select: { id: true, reputation: true } })).map((u) => [u.id, u.reputation]));
  const likes: Prisma.PhotoLikeCreateManyInput[] = [];
  for (const ph of photos) {
    if (!ph.shared || ph.removedAt) continue;
    const count = boosted.get(ph.id) ?? (r.chance(0.2) ? r.int(3, 11) : r.chance(0.5) ? r.int(1, 3) : 0);
    const latest = Math.min(now.getTime(), ph.createdAt.getTime() + 3 * HOUR_MS);
    const pool = regulars.filter((u) => u.id !== ph.userId && at(u) < ph.createdAt.getTime() && (!boosted.has(ph.id) || (reps.get(u.id) ?? 0) >= 0.5));
    for (const u of r.shuffle(pool).slice(0, count)) {
      likes.push({ userId: u.id, photoId: ph.id, createdAt: new Date(ph.createdAt.getTime() + r.int(1, Math.max(1, Math.floor((latest - ph.createdAt.getTime()) / MINUTE_MS))) * MINUTE_MS) });
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < likes.length; i += 2000) await db.photoLike.createMany({ data: likes.slice(i, i + 2000) });

  // ---------------- Featuring: the real job, once per past week, then as of now ----------------
  let featured = 0;
  for (let night = addNights(firstNight, 6); night < tonight; night = addNights(night, 7)) {
    // As if run at 5:15am after that week's last night, but never later than the anchor.
    const runAt = new Date(Math.min(nightBounds(night).end.getTime() + 10 * MINUTE_MS, now.getTime()));
    featured += (await runFeaturing(db, runAt)).featured;
  }
  featured += (await runFeaturing(db, now)).featured;

  // A moderator hides last month's top photo at one venue: the next-ranked visible photo takes its place.
  const top = await db.featuredPhoto.findFirst({
    where: { rank: 1, photoId: { not: demoStar.id }, night: { startsWith: lastMonthNight.slice(0, 7) } },
    orderBy: [{ night: "asc" }, { venueId: "asc" }],
  });
  if (top) {
    const hiddenAt = new Date(now.getTime() - 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS);
    await db.photo.update({ where: { id: top.photoId }, data: { hiddenAt } });
    await db.moderationEvent.create({ data: { id: id("me"), action: "HIDE", targetType: "PHOTO", targetId: top.photoId, actorId: mod.id, note: "Not taken at this venue (seed)", createdAt: hiddenAt } });
  }

  const regularHandles = regularPlan.map(([u, slug]) => `@${u.handle} at ${slug}`).join(", ");
  console.log(`[seed] social: ${publicIds.size} public accounts, ${requests.length} pending requests (${onDemo} on @${demo.handle}), ${photos.length} posts, ${likes.length} likes, ${featured} featured`);
  console.log(`[seed] social: verified ${verified.map((u) => `@${u.handle}`).join(", ")}; Regulars ${regularHandles}`);
  console.log(`[seed] social: @${anon.handle} is private; their shared photo at velvet-anchor (${anonTonight.id}) reads "Anonymous" to non-followers (@${fan.handle} follows them and sees the handle)`);
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/services/osm-import.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { assignSlugs, neighborhoodOf, parseOverpass, type OverpassElement } from "@/lib/osm";

export type ImportResult = { fetched: number; created: number; updated: number; unchanged: number; skippedClaimed: number };

/**
 * Idempotent upsert on osm_id, in one transaction (all or nothing).
 * - New venues start unclaimed with no official cover and no hours ("No official cover posted" / "Hours not posted").
 * - Slugs are assigned once, from OSM data alone (see assignSlugs), and never change afterwards.
 * - On re-import, coordinates always follow OSM (they define the geofence) and the neighborhood is recomputed from
 *   them. Name and address are refreshed only for unclaimed venues: once claimed, the owner owns those facts.
 * - Venues that disappear from OSM are left alone (they may have reports, photos and comments).
 */
export async function importOsmVenues(db: Db, elements: readonly OverpassElement[]): Promise<ImportResult> {
  const venues = parseOverpass(elements);
  return db.$transaction(
    async (tx) => {
      const existing = await tx.venue.findMany({
        select: { id: true, slug: true, osmId: true, name: true, address: true, neighborhood: true, lat: true, lng: true, claims: { where: { status: "APPROVED" }, select: { id: true } } },
      });
      const byOsm = new Map(existing.filter((v) => v.osmId).map((v) => [v.osmId!, v]));
      const reserved = new Set(existing.filter((v) => !v.osmId).map((v) => v.slug));
      const slugs = assignSlugs(venues, reserved);
      const usedSlugs = new Set(existing.map((v) => v.slug));
      const result: ImportResult = { fetched: venues.length, created: 0, updated: 0, unchanged: 0, skippedClaimed: 0 };

      for (const v of venues) {
        const current = byOsm.get(v.osmId);
        if (!current) {
          const slug = slugs.get(v.osmId)!;
          if (usedSlugs.has(slug)) throw new Error(`Slug collision for ${v.osmId}: "${slug}" is already used`);
          usedSlugs.add(slug);
          await tx.venue.create({
            data: { slug, osmId: v.osmId, name: v.name, address: v.address, neighborhood: v.neighborhood, lat: v.lat, lng: v.lng, hours: [], officialCoverCents: null },
          });
          result.created++;
          continue;
        }
        const claimed = current.claims.length > 0;
        const data: { lat?: number; lng?: number; neighborhood?: string; name?: string; address?: string } = {};
        if (current.lat !== v.lat || current.lng !== v.lng) {
          data.lat = v.lat;
          data.lng = v.lng;
        }
        const hood = neighborhoodOf(v.lat, v.lng) ?? current.neighborhood;
        if (hood !== current.neighborhood) data.neighborhood = hood;
        const namesDiffer = current.name !== v.name || current.address !== v.address;
        if (namesDiffer && !claimed) Object.assign(data, { name: v.name, address: v.address });
        if (namesDiffer && claimed) result.skippedClaimed++;
        if (Object.keys(data).length) {
          await tx.venue.update({ where: { id: current.id }, data });
          result.updated++;
        } else result.unchanged++;
      }
      return result;
    },
    { timeout: 120_000, maxWait: 10_000 },
  );
}


====================================================================================================
== src/server/cron.ts
====================================================================================================

import { timingSafeEqual, createHash } from "node:crypto";

/** Constant-time check of `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` (the header Vercel Cron sends). */
export function isAuthorizedCron(req: Request, secret = process.env.CRON_SECRET): boolean {
  if (!secret || secret.length < 16) return false;
  const header = req.headers.get("authorization") ?? "";
  const given = header.startsWith("Bearer ") ? header.slice(7) : "";
  const a = createHash("sha256").update(given).digest();
  const b = createHash("sha256").update(secret).digest();
  return timingSafeEqual(a, b) && given.length > 0;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/storage/index.ts
====================================================================================================

import { createHmac, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { GetObjectCommand, PutObjectCommand, DeleteObjectCommand, S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";

/**
 * Storage for uploaded images. Photos are never public: clients only ever get short-lived signed GET URLs,
 * issued by the service layer for content the viewer is allowed to see.
 *
 * - LocalDiskStorage (development/tests): files under UPLOAD_DIR, served by /api/uploads/<key>?exp=&sig=
 *   with an HMAC signature (UPLOAD_URL_SECRET).
 * - R2Storage (preview/production): Cloudflare R2 via its S3-compatible API, presigned GET URLs.
 * Selected by STORAGE_DRIVER=local|r2.
 */
export interface BlobStorage {
  put(key: string, data: Buffer, contentType: string): Promise<void>;
  get(key: string): Promise<Buffer | null>;
  delete(key: string): Promise<void>;
  /** A GET URL valid for at least `minValidSeconds`. */
  signedUrl(key: string, now?: Date): Promise<string>;
}

/** Signed URLs are valid for at least 15 minutes. */
export const SIGNED_URL_MIN_TTL_S = 15 * 60;
/**
 * Signing time is floored to 5-minute buckets and the TTL is 20 minutes, so every URL is valid for 15 to 20
 * minutes. A photo keeps the same URL within a bucket, which lets browsers and the iOS image cache reuse it
 * across the 60-second refreshes.
 */
const BUCKET_MS = 5 * 60_000;
export const SIGNED_URL_TTL_S = SIGNED_URL_MIN_TTL_S + BUCKET_MS / 1000;
export function signingInstant(now: Date): Date {
  return new Date(Math.floor(now.getTime() / BUCKET_MS) * BUCKET_MS);
}

const KEY_RE = /^[a-z0-9/_-]+\.(jpg|png|webp)$/;
export function isValidKey(key: string) {
  return KEY_RE.test(key) && !key.includes("..");
}

function uploadSecret(): string {
  const s = process.env.UPLOAD_URL_SECRET;
  if (s && s.length >= 32) return s;
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") throw new Error("UPLOAD_URL_SECRET (>= 32 chars) is required in production");
  return "dev-only-upload-url-secret-not-for-production-use";
}

function localSignature(key: string, exp: number) {
  return createHmac("sha256", uploadSecret()).update(`${key}\n${exp}`).digest("base64url");
}

/** Verifies a local signed URL's exp/sig. */
export function verifyLocalSignature(key: string, exp: string | null, sig: string | null, now = new Date()): boolean {
  if (!exp || !sig || !/^\d{10}$/.test(exp)) return false;
  if (Number(exp) * 1000 <= now.getTime()) return false;
  const expected = Buffer.from(localSignature(key, Number(exp)));
  const given = Buffer.from(sig);
  return expected.length === given.length && timingSafeEqual(expected, given);
}

export class LocalDiskStorage implements BlobStorage {
  constructor(private readonly root: string) {}

  private resolve(key: string): string {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    const full = path.resolve(this.root, key);
    if (!full.startsWith(path.resolve(this.root) + path.sep)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    return full;
  }

  async put(key: string, data: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    const full = this.resolve(key);
    await mkdir(path.dirname(full), { recursive: true });
    await writeFile(full, data);
  }

  async delete(key: string): Promise<void> {
    await rm(this.resolve(key), { force: true });
  }

  async get(key: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
    try {
      return await readFile(this.resolve(key));
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async signedUrl(key: string, now = new Date()): Promise<string> {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    const exp = Math.floor(signingInstant(now).getTime() / 1000) + SIGNED_URL_TTL_S;
    return `/api/uploads/${key}?exp=${exp}&sig=${localSignature(key, exp)}`;
  }
}

export type R2Config = { accountId: string; accessKeyId: string; secretAccessKey: string; bucket: string };

export class R2Storage implements BlobStorage {
  readonly client: S3Client;
  constructor(private readonly cfg: R2Config, client?: S3Client) {
    this.client =
      client ??
      new S3Client({
        region: "auto",
        endpoint: `https://${cfg.accountId}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
        credentials: { accessKeyId: cfg.accessKeyId, secretAccessKey: cfg.secretAccessKey },
      });
  }

  async put(key: string, data: Buffer, contentType: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    await this.client.send(
      new PutObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key, Body: data, ContentType: contentType, CacheControl: "private, max-age=900" }),
    );
  }

  async get(key: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
    try {
      const res = await this.client.send(new GetObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key }));
      return res.Body ? Buffer.from(await res.Body.transformToByteArray()) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async delete(key: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.client.send(new DeleteObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key }));
  }

  async signedUrl(key: string, now = new Date()): Promise<string> {
    if (!isValidKey(key)) throw new Error("Invalid storage key");
    return getSignedUrl(this.client, new GetObjectCommand({ Bucket: this.cfg.bucket, Key: key }), {
      expiresIn: SIGNED_URL_TTL_S,
      signingDate: signingInstant(now),
    });
  }
}

export function storageFromEnv(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): BlobStorage {
  const driver = (env.STORAGE_DRIVER ?? "local").toLowerCase();
  if (driver === "r2") {
    const cfg = {
      accountId: env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID ?? "",
      accessKeyId: env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID ?? "",
      secretAccessKey: env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY ?? "",
      bucket: env.R2_BUCKET ?? "",
    };
    const names: Record<keyof R2Config, string> = { accountId: "R2_ACCOUNT_ID", accessKeyId: "R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID", secretAccessKey: "R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY", bucket: "R2_BUCKET" };
    const missing = (Object.keys(cfg) as (keyof R2Config)[]).filter((k) => !cfg[k]).map((k) => names[k]);
    if (missing.length) throw new Error(`STORAGE_DRIVER=r2 but missing: ${missing.join(", ")}`);
    return new R2Storage(cfg);
  }
  if (driver !== "local") throw new Error(`Unknown STORAGE_DRIVER: ${driver}`);
  if (env.VERCEL === "1") throw new Error("STORAGE_DRIVER=local can't work on Vercel (read-only, ephemeral disk). Use r2.");
  return new LocalDiskStorage(path.resolve(process.cwd(), env.UPLOAD_DIR ?? "uploads"));
}

let storage: BlobStorage | null = null;
export function getStorage(): BlobStorage {
  storage ??= storageFromEnv();
  return storage;
}
export function setStorageForTests(s: BlobStorage) {
  storage = s;
}

export const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
export type ImageKind = { mime: "image/jpeg" | "image/png" | "image/webp"; ext: "jpg" | "png" | "webp" };

/** Detect image type from magic bytes. The client-declared Content-Type is never trusted. */
export function sniffImage(buf: Buffer): ImageKind | null {
  if (buf.length >= 3 && buf[0] === 0xff && buf[1] === 0xd8 && buf[2] === 0xff) return { mime: "image/jpeg", ext: "jpg" };
  if (buf.length >= 8 && buf.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a])))
    return { mime: "image/png", ext: "png" };
  if (buf.length >= 12 && buf.toString("ascii", 0, 4) === "RIFF" && buf.toString("ascii", 8, 12) === "WEBP")
    return { mime: "image/webp", ext: "webp" };
  return null;
}

export function newStorageKey(prefix: "photos" | "official" | "avatars", ext: ImageKind["ext"]): string {
  return `${prefix}/${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}.${ext}`;
}

export function mimeForKey(key: string): string {
  if (key.endsWith(".png")) return "image/png";
  if (key.endsWith(".webp")) return "image/webp";
  return "image/jpeg";
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/content-filter/index.ts
====================================================================================================

import { SUBSTRING, WHOLE_WORD } from "./wordlist";

const LEET: Record<string, string> = { "0": "o", "1": "i", "!": "i", "|": "i", "3": "e", "4": "a", "@": "a", "5": "s", "$": "s", "7": "t", "+": "t", "8": "b", "9": "g" };

/** Lowercase, strip accents and map leetspeak. */
function normalize(text: string): string {
  return text
    .normalize("NFKD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[0-9!|@$+]/g, (c) => LEET[c] ?? c);
}

/**
 * A term as a pattern of letter runs: each run of k identical letters becomes `x{k,}`. So stretched spellings
 * ("faaaggggot") still match, but a term never matches a *different* word ("coon" won't match "con"/"cons",
 * "nigger" won't match "Nigerian").
 */
function pattern(term: string): string {
  return term.replace(/([a-z])\1*/g, (run, ch: string) => `${ch}{${run.length},}`);
}
const WHOLE_RE = new RegExp(`^(?:${WHOLE_WORD.map(pattern).join("|")})$`);
const SUB_RE = new RegExp(SUBSTRING.map(pattern).join("|"));

/**
 * Letter tokens. Runs of single letters ("f a g", "w h o r e") are spelled-out words, so every contiguous piece
 * of such a run (3+ letters) is also checked; that catches "what a w h o r e" even though "a" joins the run.
 */
function tokens(norm: string): string[] {
  const raw = norm.split(/[^a-z]+/).filter(Boolean);
  const out = [...raw];
  const runs: string[] = [];
  let run = "";
  for (const t of raw) {
    if (t.length === 1) run += t;
    else {
      if (run.length > 1) runs.push(run);
      run = "";
    }
  }
  if (run.length > 1) runs.push(run);
  for (const r of runs) for (let i = 0; i < r.length; i++) for (let j = i + 3; j <= r.length; j++) out.push(r.slice(i, j));
  return out;
}

export function containsObjectionable(text: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!text) return false;
  const toks = tokens(normalize(text));
  return toks.some((t) => WHOLE_RE.test(t) || SUB_RE.test(t));
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/content-filter/wordlist.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Server-side list of slurs and sexually explicit terms that are rejected in user-visible text
 * (App Store Guideline 1.2). Deliberately small and high-precision: ordinary profanity is allowed (this is
 * a nightlife app), hate speech and explicit sexual terms are not. Moderators handle everything else.
 *
 * WHOLE_WORD terms match a complete token after normalization, so "cocktail", "spicy", "raccoon",
 * "Scunthorpe" and "fire retardant" are fine. SUBSTRING terms are long and unambiguous enough to also be
 * caught inside a single run-together token such as a handle ("xx<term>xx"), never across word breaks.
 *
 * Deliberately NOT listed: words with common innocent uses ("cock" as in Cock & Bull, "cum" as in cum laude),
 * and "rape": people must be able to warn others about assault at a venue. Moderators handle abuse of these.
 */
export const WHOLE_WORD: readonly string[] = [
  // racial and ethnic slurs
  "nigger", "niggers", "nigga", "niggas", "chink", "chinks", "gook", "gooks", "spic", "spics", "kike", "kikes",
  "wetback", "wetbacks", "raghead", "ragheads", "towelhead", "towelheads", "coon", "coons", "beaner", "beaners",
  "paki", "pakis", "zipperhead",
  // homophobic / transphobic slurs
  "fag", "fags", "faggot", "faggots", "dyke", "dykes", "tranny", "trannies", "shemale",
  // ableist slurs
  "retard", "retards", "retarded",
  // sexually explicit terms
  "cunt", "cunts", "pussy", "pussies", "blowjob", "blowjobs", "handjob", "handjobs",
  "cumshot", "cumshots", "porn", "porno", "whore", "whores", "slut", "sluts", "dildo", "jizz",
];

export const SUBSTRING: readonly string[] = ["nigger", "nigga", "faggot", "wetback", "towelhead", "raghead", "blowjob", "cumshot"];

== src/lib/osm.ts

/**
 * OpenStreetMap venue import: pure parsing. Data © OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0.
 * Pulls `amenity=bar|pub|nightclub` in the East Village + Lower East Side via Overpass.
 */
export const OSM_ATTRIBUTION = {
  text: "© OpenStreetMap contributors",
  url: "https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright",
  license: "ODbL-1.0",
} as const;

/** South, west, north, east: a superset of both neighborhoods; parseOverpass keeps only points inside them. */
export const EV_LES_BBOX = [40.7115, -73.9945, 40.7345, -73.972] as const;

type Pt = readonly [lat: number, lng: number];
/**
 * Neighborhood outlines (approximate, drawn from street intersections, checked against a live Overpass pull):
 * - East Village: 14th St (both sides) to Houston St; the Bowery frontage, Cooper Square and 4th Ave (north of Astor Pl)
 *   to Avenue D. Lafayette St, Broadway and University Pl (NoHo / the Village) are outside.
 * - Lower East Side: Houston St to Canal St / East Broadway; the Bowery frontage (incl. Chrystie and Forsyth) to the FDR.
 *   Chinatown / Two Bridges (south-west of Canal & East Broadway) is outside.
 * Houston St is a polyline through its real intersections because it bends.
 */
const HOUSTON: readonly Pt[] = [
  [40.7248, -73.9932], // Bowery (west side)
  [40.7236, -73.9904], // 2nd Ave
  [40.7224, -73.9868], // Avenue A
  [40.7213, -73.9837], // Avenue B
  [40.7203, -73.9808], // Avenue C
  [40.7193, -73.9781], // Avenue D
  [40.7185, -73.975], // FDR Drive
];
export const EAST_VILLAGE: readonly Pt[] = [
  [40.7351, -73.9901], // 14th St (north frontage) & 4th Ave
  [40.7317, -73.9818], // 14th St (north frontage) & 1st Ave
  [40.7297, -73.976], // 14th St (north frontage) & Avenue C/D
  ...[...HOUSTON].reverse(), // Houston St, east to west
  [40.7274, -73.9928], // Bowery (west side) & E 4th St
  [40.7299, -73.9908], // Astor Place / Cooper Square
  [40.7351, -73.9903], // 4th Ave up to 14th St
];
export const LOWER_EAST_SIDE: readonly Pt[] = [
  ...HOUSTON, // Houston St, west to east
  [40.7131, -73.9776], // Grand St & FDR
  [40.7133, -73.9895], // East Broadway & Rutgers St
  [40.7166, -73.9962], // Canal St & Bowery (west side)
];

/** Ray-casting point-in-polygon on lat/lng (fine at neighborhood scale). */
export function inPolygon([lat, lng]: Pt, poly: readonly Pt[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [yi, xi] = poly[i]!;
    const [yj, xj] = poly[j]!;
    if (yi > lat !== yj > lat && lng < ((xj - xi) * (lat - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

export function neighborhoodOf(lat: number, lng: number): "East Village" | "Lower East Side" | null {
  if (inPolygon([lat, lng], EAST_VILLAGE)) return "East Village";
  if (inPolygon([lat, lng], LOWER_EAST_SIDE)) return "Lower East Side";
  return null;
}

export function overpassQuery(bbox: readonly [number, number, number, number] = EV_LES_BBOX) {
  const b = bbox.join(",");
  return `[out:json][timeout:60];(nwr["amenity"~"^(bar|pub|nightclub)$"](${b}););out center tags;`;
}

export type OverpassElement = {
  type: "node" | "way" | "relation";
  id: number;
  lat?: number;
  lon?: number;
  center?: { lat: number; lon: number };
  tags?: Record<string, string>;
};

export type OsmVenue = { osmId: string; name: string; address: string; neighborhood: "East Village" | "Lower East Side"; lat: number; lng: number; kind: string };

export function slugify(name: string): string {
  return (
    name
      .normalize("NFKD")
      .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/&/g, " and ")
      .replace(/['’]/g, "")
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
      .slice(0, 60) || "venue"
  );
}

const M_PER_DEG = 111_195;
function closeTogether(a: { lat: number; lng: number }, b: { lat: number; lng: number }, meters: number) {
  const dLat = (a.lat - b.lat) * M_PER_DEG;
  const dLng = (a.lng - b.lng) * M_PER_DEG * Math.cos((a.lat * Math.PI) / 180);
  return Math.hypot(dLat, dLng) <= meters;
}
const normName = (n: string) => slugify(n).replace(/-/g, "");

/**
 * Parses Overpass elements into venues: named bars/pubs/nightclubs with coordinates inside the East Village or
 * Lower East Side outlines. OSM sometimes maps one venue twice (a node and a building way): elements with the
 * same normalized name within 60 m collapse to one, chosen deterministically (has a street address, then node
 * over way over relation, then lowest id). Addresses come from addr:* tags; when absent the address is "".
 * Sorted by osmId.
 */
export function parseOverpass(elements: readonly OverpassElement[]): OsmVenue[] {
  const candidates: (OsmVenue & { rank: [number, number, number] })[] = [];
  for (const el of elements) {
    const tags = el.tags ?? {};
    const name = (tags.name ?? "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    const lat = el.lat ?? el.center?.lat;
    const lng = el.lon ?? el.center?.lon;
    if (!name || lat == null || lng == null) continue;
    if (!["bar", "pub", "nightclub"].includes(tags.amenity ?? "")) continue;
    const neighborhood = neighborhoodOf(lat, lng);
    if (!neighborhood) continue;
    const street = [tags["addr:housenumber"], tags["addr:street"]].filter(Boolean).join(" ").trim().slice(0, 120);
    candidates.push({
      osmId: `${el.type}/${el.id}`, name: name.slice(0, 80), address: street, neighborhood, lat, lng, kind: tags.amenity!,
      rank: [street ? 0 : 1, el.type === "node" ? 0 : el.type === "way" ? 1 : 2, el.id],
    });
  }
  candidates.sort((a, b) => a.rank[0] - b.rank[0] || a.rank[1] - b.rank[1] || a.rank[2] - b.rank[2]);
  const kept: typeof candidates = [];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (!kept.some((k) => normName(k.name) === normName(c.name) && closeTogether(k, c, 60))) kept.push(c);
  }
  return kept
    .map((c): OsmVenue => ({ osmId: c.osmId, name: c.name, address: c.address, neighborhood: c.neighborhood, lat: c.lat, lng: c.lng, kind: c.kind }))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.osmId < b.osmId ? -1 : a.osmId > b.osmId ? 1 : 0));
}

/**
 * Deterministic slugs from the OSM data itself: the name's slug if no other venue in the import shares it, else
 * `<slug>-<n|w|r><id>` for every venue with that name. `reserved` holds slugs owned by non-OSM venues (demo or
 * manually created), which also force the suffix. Already-imported venues keep their slug forever (see import).
 */
export function assignSlugs(venues: readonly OsmVenue[], reserved: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set()): Map<string, string> {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const v of venues) counts.set(slugify(v.name), (counts.get(slugify(v.name)) ?? 0) + 1);
  const out = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const v of venues) {
    const base = slugify(v.name);
    const [type, id] = v.osmId.split("/") as [string, string];
    out.set(v.osmId, counts.get(base)! > 1 || reserved.has(base) ? `${base}-${type[0]}${id}` : base);
  }
  return out;
}


====================================================================================================
== src/lib/auth/session.ts
====================================================================================================

import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import type { SessionClient } from "@prisma/client";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db";
import { DAY_MS } from "@/lib/time";

export const SESSION_COOKIE = "lc_session";
/** Fixed (non-sliding) lifetimes: the cookie (or keychain token) and the DB row expire together. */
export const SESSION_TTL_MS = 30 * DAY_MS;
/** iOS tokens live in the keychain; a longer fixed lifetime avoids monthly forced sign-outs. */
export const IOS_SESSION_TTL_MS = 90 * DAY_MS;

export function hashToken(token: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
}

export async function createSession(db: Db, userId: string, opts: { client?: SessionClient; now?: Date } = {}) {
  const client = opts.client ?? "WEB";
  const now = opts.now ?? new Date();
  const token = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
  const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + (client === "IOS" ? IOS_SESSION_TTL_MS : SESSION_TTL_MS));
  await db.session.create({ data: { id: hashToken(token), userId, client, expiresAt, createdAt: now } });
  return { token, expiresAt, client };
}

/**
 * Resolves a session token to its user. When `client` is given, the session must have been issued for that
 * channel: IOS tokens only work as bearer tokens and WEB tokens only as the cookie, so a leaked 90-day iOS
 * token can't be replayed as a browser cookie (and vice versa).
 */
export async function getSessionUser(
  db: Db,
  token: string | undefined | null,
  opts: { client?: SessionClient; now?: Date } = {},
) {
  const now = opts.now ?? new Date();
  if (!token || token.length > 200) return null;
  const session = await db.session.findUnique({ where: { id: hashToken(token) }, include: { user: true } });
  if (!session) return null;
  if (opts.client && session.client !== opts.client) return null;
  if (session.user.deletedAt || session.user.suspendedAt) return null;
  if (session.expiresAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) {
    await db.session.deleteMany({ where: { id: session.id } });
    return null;
  }
  return session.user;
}

export async function destroySession(db: Db, token: string | undefined | null) {
  if (!token) return;
  await db.session.deleteMany({ where: { id: hashToken(token) } });
}

/** Revokes every session (web and iOS) for a user. Returns how many were revoked. */
export async function destroyAllSessions(db: Db, userId: string) {
  const { count } = await db.session.deleteMany({ where: { userId } });
  return count;
}

export function sessionCookieAttributes(expiresAt: Date) {
  return {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: "lax" as const,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    path: "/",
    expires: expiresAt,
  };
}


====================================================================================================
== prisma/seed-guard.ts
====================================================================================================

/**
 * The demo seed (and `db:reset`, which drops the database first) wipes every table and inserts fictional
 * venues and people. It must never run against production. It is allowed only when:
 *   - APP_ENV is "development" or "preview" (explicitly), and VERCEL_ENV is not "production"; or
 *   - APP_ENV is unset AND the database host is local (localhost / 127.0.0.1 / docker "db").
 * A missing APP_ENV against a remote database is refused, so a forgotten variable fails safe.
 */
export function assertSeedAllowed(env: Record<string, string | undefined>) {
  const appEnv = env.APP_ENV?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const refuse = (why: string) => {
    throw new Error(`Refusing to run the demo seed: ${why}. It wipes all tables and inserts fake data. Use \`pnpm venues:import\` for real venues.`);
  };
  if (appEnv === "production") refuse("APP_ENV=production");
  if (env.VERCEL_ENV === "production") refuse("VERCEL_ENV=production");
  if (appEnv === "preview") {
    // Preview is a public URL: seeded accounts (incl. admin/moderator) must not use the well-known dev password.
    const pw = env.DEV_SEED_PASSWORD ?? "";
    if (pw.length < 16 || pw === "lastcall-dev") refuse("APP_ENV=preview needs a strong DEV_SEED_PASSWORD (16+ chars, not the dev default)");
    if (!env.APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD || env.APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD.length < 12) refuse("APP_ENV=preview needs APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD (12+ chars) for the App Review account");
    return;
  }
  if (appEnv === "development") return;
  if (appEnv) refuse(`unknown APP_ENV "${appEnv}"`);
  let host = "";
  try {
    host = new URL(env.DIRECT_URL ?? env.DATABASE_URL ?? "").hostname;
  } catch {
    refuse("DATABASE_URL is missing or invalid");
  }
  if (!["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1", "db"].includes(host)) refuse(`APP_ENV is not set and the database (${host}) is not local`);
}

== src/middleware.ts

import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

/** Dev-only pages (seeded-account login) return a hard 404 outside development. */
export function middleware(req: NextRequest) {
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "development" && req.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith("/dev")) {
    return new NextResponse("Not found", { status: 404 });
  }
  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = { matcher: ["/dev/:path*"] };
